10 月通胀数据点评：能源链主导上游涨价，低位行业延续修复   
[Table_Author] 
 魏争 分析师  沈夏宜 分析师

Email:weizheng@lczq.com Email:shenxiayi@lczq.com  
证书:S1320524100001 证书:S1320523020004

### Page 2

宏观经济点评  
请务必阅读正文之后的信息披露和法律声明 2 
0.7%，指向工业品价格仍处上行区间，上涨速度出现放缓。结构上看，一
是输入性能源价格继续强化，化工链价格上升。 10 月中东局势扰动仍然
较强，国际能源价格升至高位，输入性价格继续传导。石油和天然气开采
业价格同比升至 35.0%（上月 24.5%），石油煤炭加工业升至 29.9%（上
月 21.6%），化学原料制品制造业升至 16.4%（上月 12.1%），化纤制造业
16.0%（上月 12.3%）；环比看，石油和天然气开采业上涨 6.0%（上月
10.4%）。二是煤炭价格维持高增，上行斜率 放缓。煤炭开采和洗选业价
格同比升至 36.6%（上月 35.3%），环比涨幅降至 2.7%（上月 9.5%）。供
给端约束叠加高油价替代效应，煤价维持高位，但经历前期快速上涨后边
际动能有所放缓。三是，有色链由前期持续走强转为降温。 有色金属矿采
选业同比降至 16.3%（上月 21.4%），有色冶炼同比降至 18.6%（上月
21.4%）。AI 产业链下游价格维持韧性，计算机通信和其他电子设备制造
业同比由 5.8%小幅升至 5.9%，电气机械和器材制造业维持 6.2%。 
生产资料与生活资料 PPI 分化延续，部分长期负增行业继续收窄。10
月，生产资料 PPI 同比上涨 7.4%，较 9 月提高 0.9 个百分点；生活资料
PPI 同比下降 0.1%，与 9 月持平，二者分化仍然明显。细分看，一方
面，部分此前长期低迷行业延续修复 。一是建筑材料链延续改善。非金制
品业价格同比降幅收窄至 1.6%（上月 2.5%），环比涨幅扩大至 0.8%（上
月 0.3%）；黑金冶炼和压延加工业同比上涨 0.4%（上月-0.2%），指向建
筑材料链延续了 9 月以来修复趋势，或与“反内卷”以来相关行业产能调
整、供求关系改善有关。二是多数长期负增的消费制造行业同比降幅继续
收窄。汽车制造业由-2.0%收窄至-1.8%，医药制造业由-3.3%收窄至-
3.2%，酒饮料和精制茶制造业由-5.3%收窄至-5.2%，农副食品加工业由-
0.9%收窄至-0.4%。另一方面，下游价格改善仍未形成全面扩散。 生活资
料中，衣着、一般日用品价格降幅小幅扩大，耐用消费品同比涨幅回落至
1.4%（上月 1.6%）；环比看，生活资料整体增速为-0.1%（上月 0.1%）。 
往后看，上下游价格分化存在进一步收敛的条件， 广谱性修复仍需内需接
力。一方面，中期选举后美国国内对进一步扩大中东冲突的约束有所增
强，前期推动油价快速上行的地缘风险溢价或有所回落，能源价格继续大
幅上冲的概率下降，输入性通胀对 PPI 上游价格的边际支撑可能趋弱。另
一方面，建筑材料及部分低位制造业价格已连续改善，叠加四季度政策项
目逐步形成实物工作量，黑色、建材等行业仍存在进一步修复的条件 。后
续价格结构性分化有望从两端同时收敛，一端是能源等上游行业价格上行
斜率放缓，另一端是建筑链及部分长期低迷行业逐步修复。总量上看，10
月核心 CPI 和生活资料 PPI 均未同步走强，指向当前下游修复尚未形成广
泛的内生价格动能。后续判断价格向广谱改善转变仍在于两条链条，一是
黑色、建材等价格改善能否在政策需求支撑下延续并向中游制造扩散； 二
是食品加工、汽车、医药等低位行业跌幅收窄能否进一步转化为生活资料
PPI、核心 CPI 和服务价格的持续改善。

风险提示：国际油价大幅波动超预期、国内需求修复不及预期。

投资评级说明 
投资建议的评级标准  评级 说明 
评级标准为报告发布日后的 6 个月内公司股价 （或行业
指数） 相对同期基准指数的相对市场表现。 其中A 股市
场以沪深 300 指数为基准； 新三板市场以三板成指 （针
对协议转让标的） 或三板做市指数 （针对做市转让标的）
为基准；香港市场以恒生指数为基准。 
股票评级 
买入 相对同期基准指数涨幅在 10%以上 
增持 相对同期基准指数涨幅在 5%~10%之间 
中性 相对同期基准指数涨幅在-5%~+5%之间 
减持 相对同期基准指数跌幅在 5%以上 
行业评级 
看好 相对表现优于市场 
中性 相对表现与市场持平 
看淡 相对表现弱于市场

顧
市場焦點
美國
• 9月ISM服務業PMI降至54.9，雖商業活動與新訂單放緩 ，但就業回升至 50.1、訂單積壓增加 ，
顯示服務需求仍具韌性；另一方面，價格指數升至74.0，創2022年7月以來新高，供應商交貨
速度亦放緩 ，反映燃料 、原物料及供應鏈成本升高 ，景氣未明顯轉弱之際 ，通膨壓力反而重
新升溫；同時全球長債殖利率同步走高 ，AI基建投資延續企業成長動能 ，也提高資本需求 。
整體而言 ，美國經濟尚未明顯轉弱 ，但通膨與長端利率壓力同步升高 ，使金融條件維持偏緊 。
中國
• 彭博指出在政府積極推動開源策略與 AI普及下，中國產業快速發展出現供給過剩 ，產業獲利
能力面臨挑戰，未來三年中國AI產業將可能處於虧損狀態，不過即使模型價格下降，AI使用量
增加將帶動Token需求大幅成長，其中具有企業客戶、專有資料及生態優勢的AI龍頭，將比單

純提供模型服務的公司更具長期競爭力。
黃金
• 歐美國家財政可持續性的疑慮持續發酵 ，加上長期通膨風險升溫 ，宏觀不確定性讓實體黃金
市場逆勢吸金 。本波下跌主要反映去槓桿交易 ，實體黃金的基本面未變 ，有望為金價帶來中
期的支撐。建議投資人逢修正分批進場 ，可透過黃金存摺或是實體黃金 ETF(GLD)，以投資組
合多元化的角度布局。波段投機者，從技術指標來看，可留意4,000美元關卡是否出現支撐。

美國9月ISM服務需求略降、成本壓力升高，景氣韌性與通膨風險並存
• 服務業維持擴張，需求略降但就業回穩：9月ISM服務業指數降至54.9(前月55.4)，連續27個月位於擴張區間；商業活動
指數降至56.5、新訂單降至 59.8，顯示服務需求較前月放緩 ，但就業指數回升至 50.1、重返擴張區間 ，訂單積壓升至
56.6，顯示內需仍具韌性，服務業尚未出現明顯轉弱訊號。
• 成本與供應鏈壓力升高 ，通膨降溫面臨干擾： 價格指數升至 74.0，創2022年7月以來新高 ，且為7個月來第6次高於70；
供應商交貨指數升至53.2，顯示交貨速度放緩，燃料、柴油、鋼材、銅及記憶體等成本上升，加上供應受限，使服務業
投入成本壓力擴大，通膨降溫進程面臨干擾。
• 市場觀察：美國服務業為經濟成長的重要支撐，目前經濟呈現「需求仍強、通膨升溫」的拉鋸格局，雖服務業景氣支撐

美國企業獲利，但成本上升可能推升利率波動，投資上宜偏向美國短中天期投資級債，兼顧較高收益率與利率風險控管；
股票則可聚焦具定價能力、獲利與現金流穩健的優質企業。
資料來源：Bloomberg， 2026.10.6 資料來源：ISM Services PMI Report， 2026.10.6
ISM服務業指數分項指標變動對比表ISM服務業PMI主要分項指數變化

全球利率同步走高、AI投資持續擴張，長端美債壓力仍高
• 全球長債殖利率同步走高，反映利率環境重新定價：美國財政部長貝森特指出，近期美債殖利率攀升與德國、日本等主
要債市同步上行，反映全球資本成本重新定價，而非市場對美國資產失去信心；加上目前尚未出現資金大規模撤離美債
並轉向其他主權債券的現象，因此債市波動較偏向全球利率環境調整，而非金融系統性風險。
• AI投資增加資金需求，支撐較高利率環境：貝森特亦駁斥AI泡沫論，認為微軟、Alphabet及Meta等大型科技企業仍以
實際獲利與現金流支撐持續擴大AI資本支出，不同於過去缺乏基本面的科技泡沫；龐大的資料中心 、電力與半導體投資
需求提高整體資金成本，也成為推升長天期利率的重要因素之一。
• 市場觀察：目前市場經濟近況屬於利率高檔、成長尚穩的情境中，因而投資策略建議配置大型科技股、AI基建供應鏈及

受惠利差環境的金融股具相對優勢；債券配置則以中短天期投資級債優先，避免長天期債券受利率上行壓力侵蝕。
資料來源：Bloomberg， 2026.10.6

獲利成長；中長期建議維持日股正向看法，聚焦金融、AI、機器人、自動化及電力設備等具結構性成長題材的企業。

中國AI產業陷入逐底競爭困境
• 中國AI產業陷入逐底競爭困境 ：彭博指出在政府積極推動開源策略與
AI普及下，中國AI產業快速發展 ，但也逐漸出現供給過剩 ，使產業獲
利能力面臨挑戰 。開源模式雖有助於降低技術門檻 、應用普及 ，但也
使技術優勢難以長期維持 ，加上中國市場長期習慣低價甚至免費的數
位服務，多數AI業者難以透過訂閱模式獲利，轉而依賴利潤較低的API
服務作為主要收入來源 。目前中國已備案大型語言模型 (LLM)數量達
988個，包括DeepSeek、Kimi及智譜AI等新創企業快速崛起，進一步
加劇市場競爭。此外API服務逐漸被視為基礎公用服務，即使AI Agent
帶動詞元(Token)需求快速成長，也未必能同步提升獲利，由於開源模
型可由不同雲端業者部署 ，市場進入門檻降低 ，業者更難建立長期競

爭優勢。
• 後市展望： 考量龐大資本支出與持續價格競爭 ，未來三年中國 AI產業
整體仍可能處於虧損狀態 ，市場將形成「美國閉源、高毛利」與「中
國開源 、低成本 」模式 ，即使模型價格下降 ，AI 使用量增加將帶動
Token需求大幅成長 ，進而推升資料中心 、雲端服務及 AI晶片需求 ，
從投資角度來看，基礎設施供應商(晶片、伺服器、資料中心、雲端)受
惠程度可能高於模型開發商；而具有企業客戶 、專有資料及生態優勢
的AI龍頭，將比單純提供模型服務的公司更具長期競爭力。

中國已備案的LLM數量

人行下調抵押補充貸款利率和房貸貼息等政策出台，有望提振中國經濟
和市場信心
• 人行下調抵押補充貸款利率和房貸貼息等政策出台 ，有望提振中國經濟和市場信心 ：日前中國推出購房貸款貼息政策及下調
PSL(抵押補充貸款)利率，代表穩增長政策進一步加碼 ，有助於提振市場信心與經濟預期 ，此次政策不僅支持房地產市場 ，也
將「六張網」建設、科技創新、民營及小微企業 ，納入重點扶持範圍 ，透過增加再貸款額度 ，與提高支持比例 ，帶動投資、
就業及消費回升。短期A股有望在政策推動下出現築底反彈，其中基建投資、製造業投資、房市銷售及居民消費有機會改善，
同時PSL(抵押補充貸款)降息有助於流動性環境邊際寬鬆 ，降低市場對經濟增長的疑慮 ，後市來看企業獲利與信用環境有望逐
步修復，進一步鞏固A股慢牛格局。投資建議上房貸貼息政策有利房地產、建材、家居等產業鏈，金融業受惠資金成本下降與

信貸回升，食品飲料、零售、家電及服務消費則可望受惠於居民消費意願提升。

中國、日本減持美國公債的背後邏輯與差異
• 橋水基金創始人達利歐警告 ，美國公債正面臨中日兩國減持的風險 ，數據顯示這項警告已成事實 。2026年前七個月，中國
美國公債持倉減少660億美元至6,180億美元；同期日本持倉亦減少820億美元至1.1兆美元，扣除價格變動的估值效應後，
兩國實質持倉均大幅下滑。然而，兩國削減美國公債的背後動機並不相同，這將導致未來的減持軌跡出現明顯分化。
• 中國戰略性長期去美元化：1.降低地緣政治風險與轉向資產多元化 ，隨著人民幣在國際貿易結算中使用增加 ，市場流動性提
升，中國對美元儲備依賴度顯著下降。2.美債財政可持續性隱憂，美國政府債務持續攀升，引發對美國長期財政健康疑慮。
3.根據彭博經濟研究模型預測，在漸進式減持情境下，中國的美元儲備(以美債與機構債為主)到2050年可能減少約50%。

• 中國搭配戰術性節奏調整 (短期緩和因素)：儘管長期趨勢向下 ，但目前中美利差顯著 (美中30年期公債利差超過 350個基點)，
在美債殖利率維持高位期間，過快減持意味著實現債券價格下跌的虧損，且可能加劇人民幣升值壓力。因此，中國短中期內
傾向採取「戰略性減持、戰術性放緩」的靈活步調。
美國公債持倉：中國 vs 日本
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.10.8，美國財政部
中國預計將減少美元儲備

日本受政策利率與本土資本回流驅動
• 與中國的結構性考量不同，日本的減持主要出自週期性因素與匯率干預需求。
• 日本央行貨幣政策轉鷹： 日本央行正加快升息腳步 。隨著日債殖利率上揚 (30年期日債殖利率升至約 4.2%)，美日利差持續
收窄，吸引日本國內機構資金回流本土主權債市場。
• 匯率干預政策：日本為防範日圓過度貶值，日本財務省傾向出售部分海外美債資產以籌集外匯干預資金。若日圓再次面臨壓
力，此類拋售干預可能持續。因此，隨著日本央行逐步走向政策正常化，日本對美債的持倉大概會延續下滑軌跡。
• 小結與投資建議：美國公債作為全球無風險資產的地位正面臨結構性挑戰，中國的「長期去美元化」與日本的「本土利率回
升資金回流」將形成雙重壓力，這意味著美債殖利率短期內難大幅下行，美國財政部發債成本居高不下，進而推高全球資本

的融資成本。投資債券配置建議採取「中短天期＋階梯式配置」策略，長天期美國公債(20年以上)受發債供給過剩及中日兩
國長期減持影響，價格波動較大。建議投資人適度降低長天期美國公債暴險，轉向防禦性較佳的中短天期美國公債或高品質
高評級公司債，以鎖定當前較高殖利率同時控管利率風險。
殖利率差：美元與人民幣 vs 美元與日圓
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.29

黃金市場資金流向分歧，實體投資有望支撐中長期金價表現
• 利率與匯率因素，壓抑金價近期表現：9月以來，美國公債殖利率飆升、美元指數走揚，由於黃金本身不孳息，資金傾向轉
往固定收益市場，疊加美元走強壓抑國際購買意願，金價自8月底高點至今已下修約11%。
• 9月資金流向分歧，實體投資強、期貨市場弱：根據世界黃金協會統計，9月在金價下跌之際，全球實體黃金ETF卻吸引大量
資金流入，流入約100億美元(67噸)。另一方面，COMEX黃金期貨市場投機部位大幅下降 ，避險基金與其他機構投資人的
多頭部位明顯減少，造成期貨市場賣壓增加，進一步推動金價下跌。
• 實體黃金市場逆勢吸金反應投資人的避險需求：歐美國家財政可持續性的疑慮持續發酵，加上美伊衝突未解及AI算力需求強
勁，長期通膨風險升溫，宏觀的不確定性讓實體黃金市場逆勢吸金。從數據上來看，本波下跌主要反映去槓桿交易，實體黃

金的基本面未變 ，有望為金價帶來中期的支撐 。建議投資人逢修正分批進場 ，可透過黃金存摺或是實體黃金 ETF(GLD)，以
投資組合多元化的角度布局。波段投機者，從技術指標來看，可留意4,000美元關卡是否出現支撐。
資料來源：世界黃金協會，2026.10.7。

1. 本資料內容僅供內部同仁及客戶參考，非作為任何金融商品或服務之購買或銷售之推介，不得視為買賣有價證券或其他金融商品之要約或要約之引誘。
2. 本資料提及之經濟走勢預測不必然代表基金之績效，基金投資風險請詳閱基金公開說明書。
3. 基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責本基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益 ，投資人申購
前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 本資料所載內容如涉及商品資訊 ，商品相關資訊仍以經核可之條款及文宣 (DM)為主，本行網站已備有公開說明書可供查閱基金應負擔之費用及相關投資風險 。投
資人申購基金係持有基金受益憑證，而非本資料提及之投資資產或標的。
5. 本資料所載內容取自本公司相信可靠之資料來源，且為特定日期之判斷，本公司不保證其即時性、精確性及完整性，亦不提供或嘗試遊說客戶做為投資之判斷依據。

資料內容邇後若有變更，本公司將不做預告或主動更新。投資人於決策時應審慎衡量本身風險，如因信賴本資料內容而做出或改變投資決策，須自行承擔結果，本
公司及本公司所屬員工，均不負任何損害賠償及其他法律上之責任。
6. 本資料內容之著作權屬兆豐銀行所有，禁止就所載資料任何形式之抄襲、引用或轉載。
7. 本資料所載部分基金涉及非投資等級債，投資人投資以非投資等級債券為訴求之基金不宜占其投資組合過高之比重 。基金經金管會核准，惟不表示絕無風險。由於
非投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，故本基金可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支
付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損。部分基金不適合無法承擔相關風險之投資人。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良
管理人之注意義務外不負責本基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
8. 基金年化配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除息日前一日之淨值×一年配息次數×100%。「年化配息率」為估算值。當月配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除

息日前一日之淨值×100%。
9. 基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動。投資人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績
效亦不代表未來績效之保證。本資料如以基金過去績效進行模擬投資組合之報酬率時，僅為歷史資料模擬投資組合之結果，不代表本投資組合之實際報酬率及未來
績效保證，不同時間進行模擬操作，其結果亦可能不同。
10. 部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之相關費用。基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部份，可能導致原始投資金額減損。各基金由本
金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至各投信基金公司及總代理人網站查詢。
11. 屬基金資訊觀測站ESG基金專區所列之基金，有關基金之ESG資訊，投資人應於申購前詳閱基金公開說明書或投資人須知所載之基金所有特色或目標等資訊。

市場焦點
美國
• 9月CPI年增3.7%、PPI年增6.3%，雙雙高於前期 ，能源價格的急升是通膨回升的主要推力 。
雖然核心CPI年增率僅升至2.6%，但住房與服務價格仍維持黏著，使通膨降溫過程出現反覆；
同時PPI增幅明顯高於 CPI，反映企業成本壓力偏高 ，未來不排除進一步向終端價格傳導 。整
體而言，CPI與PPI同步回升反映通膨降溫進程仍存在不確定性 ，進而令市場重估通膨回落速
度與對Fed貨幣政策路徑的審慎預期。
AI
• AI雲端業者 CoreWeave的5年期CDS利差一度突破 1,140個基點 ，旗下兩檔債券殖利率逼近
15%，相關賣壓並進一步蔓延至股票市場 。全球最大半導體設備商ASML表示，客戶已開始洽
談2029至2030年的訂單；晶圓代工龍頭台積電財報亦優於市場預期 。與此同時，台灣出口增

速創3月以來新高，韓國9月半導體出口年增逾260%，顯示半導體需求持續強勁，預期第三季
相關企業獲利仍有望維持強勁。
中國
• 9月通膨加速主要源於中東能源衝擊及 AI硬體需求，能源和科技領域之外 ，普遍性的通縮壓力
仍在，彭博表示以GDP平減指數衡量 ，經歷三年下滑後 ，整體經濟預計將在 2026年走出通縮 ，
投資上短期可聚焦，受惠AI資本支出與能源價格上行的半導體、伺服器及能源相關族群。

美股獲利引擎轉向半導體/核心AI與能源
• 雖然標普500指數在2026年第三季展現出獲利韌性 ，但隨著油價上漲侵蝕利潤率 、消費者受通膨壓抑 ，以及分析師下調未
來預測動能，市場未來的容錯空間正在縮小。
• AI與半導體仍為獲利領頭羊：科技與半導體持續為標普500指數獲利成長的核心動力，其中約20家企業組成的核心AI一籃子
股票(權重約34.6%)對指數獲利的貢獻度已超過科技七雄(Mag 7)，且此優勢預計將至少延續至2028年底。
• 油價推升能源產業，接棒成為強棒：受到近期國際油價上揚影響，能源產業獲利預測顯著上修，預計獲利成長勁道將延續至
2027年初。在接下來的三個季度中，能源板塊的收益成長表現預計將超越科技七雄。
• 消費與運輸業面臨成本壓頂：相較於科技與能源的強勁，必需消費品與交通運輸業正承受顯著壓力。例如，達美航空受高燃

料成本拖累，而Nike與百事可樂則反映出消費者因持續通膨而縮減支出的困境。
7月以來EPS和營收成長預期修正
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.10.16

利潤率高檔盤整、關稅退款帶來短補，但動能已見放緩
• 利潤率高檔盤整，淨利潤率提升受限：標普500成分股第二季營運利潤率(18.7%)與淨利潤率(15.6%)均創下新高。雖然第三
季營運利潤率預估可進一步提升至19.4%，但淨利潤率預計微幅上升至15.8%，顯示整體企業淨利潤的擴張動能正漸趨平緩 。
• 700億美元關稅退款提供短線補藥： 受惠於美國政府依據國際緊急經濟權力法 (IEEPA)的追償退稅，估計仍有至少700億美元
的關稅款將退還給標普 500成分股(主要集中於工業、非必需消費及醫療保健等 )，這預計能為2026年標普500指數每股收益
(EPS)成長帶來約235個基點、利潤率提升約34個基點的短期提振。
• 上修動能開始轉弱：雖然整體財報指引動量仍維持正值，但過去六週內，分析師對未來12個月EPS與收入的修正動能出現頻

繁下修，這意味著市場先前的極度樂觀情緒正在消退，未來財報季度要優於預期的門檻正在提高。
• 小結與投資建議： 標普500指數短期內靠著半導體 /AI與能源雙引擎拉抬 ，加上關稅退款的一次性利多注入 ，下半年整體獲
利依然具備支撐。然而，高油價帶來的營運成本上升 ，以及終端消費力道的疲軟 ，正逐漸侵蝕非科技/非能源企業的利潤 ，
而大盤廣度不足(僅少數板塊支撐獲利)，代表未來投資風險正逐漸集中 ，但AI算力需求仍屬硬性需求，但波動加劇下，投資
建議以定期定額方式投資美股核心AI與半導體龍頭股票。

美國9月CPI、PPI 同步升溫，能源衝擊擴大通膨與利率風險
• 能源價格急升，消費通膨再升溫：美國9月CPI月增0.6%、
年增3.7%，高於前月 3.4%，其中汽油月增 9.1%、能源
價格月增 5.2%，汽油單項即貢獻整體 CPI當月漲幅逾半；
另一方面，核心CPI月增0.3%、年增2.6%、住房指數月
增0.4%，顯示能源雖是本次通膨回升主因 ，但住房及部
分服務價格仍具黏著性，令通膨降溫進程面臨反覆。
• 上游價格大幅攀升 ，成本傳導風險升高： 9月PPI最終需
求月增1.3%、年增6.3%，創2022年3月以來最大增幅，
其中最終需求商品月增 2.6%，能源價格大漲 10.8%，汽
油價格更跳升 20.3%；核心PPI月增0.5%、年增5.0%，
顯示價格壓力不僅來自能源 ，也涉及部分商品與服務環

節；後續是否進一步推升至 CPI，仍須觀察企業成本轉
嫁程度及需求變化。
• 市場看法： 通膨回升恐提高 Fed再度升息機率 ，並延長
高利率維持時間 ，甚至不排除進一步緊縮的可能 ，短期
有利支撐美債殖利率及美元表現 ，不過股、債市後續走
勢仍將受到貨幣政策預期與風險情緒變化影響 。資產配
置建議可關注具成本轉嫁能力 、現金流穩健的能源類股；
債券則宜以短中天期 、高信用評等標的為主 ，以降低利
率敏感度。黃金可作為分散地緣政治及通膨風險的輔助
配置，但不宜將任何單一資產視為必然受惠標的。
CPI 與核心 CPI 走勢變動
資料來源：U.S. Bureau of Labor Statistics，Producer Price Indexes – Sep  2026，2026.10.16

供給面推動中國9月CPI升0.9%
• 供給面推動中國9月通膨復甦：中國9月通膨數據顯示，CPI年增率由8月的0.8%升至0.9%，連續兩個月擴大，但低於市場預期
的1.0%，物價上漲主要受到能源及旅遊價格帶動，而食品價格跌幅進一步擴大，使整體通膨壓力仍相對溫和，扣除食品與能源
後，核心CPI維持在1.0%，反映內需需求尚未明顯升溫 。另一方面，PPI年增率由3.8%大幅升至5.0%，優於市場預期，且月增
率達1.2%，創4月以來最大漲幅，顯示上游原物料及工業品價格回升，須關注未來是否逐步傳導至消費端價格。
• 展望後市： 9月通膨加速主要源於中東能源衝擊及 AI硬體需求 ，能源和科技領域之外 ，普遍性的通縮壓力仍 在，彭博表示 以
GDP平減指數衡量，經歷三年下滑後，整體經濟預計將在2026年走出通縮，值得注意的是，這種供給面驅動的通膨復甦，將降

低官方推出需求刺激措施的緊迫感 ，而要反轉通縮循環正需要官方舉措，投資上短期可聚焦受惠AI資本支出與能源價格上行的
半導體、伺服器及能源相關族群。
資料來源：彭博，2026.10.14
通膨集中於能源和AI相關行業

過去18個月來，新興市場股票表現亮眼，累積報酬率超過標普500指數
的兩倍
• 過去18個月來，新興市場股票表現亮眼，累積報酬率超過標普500指數的兩倍：主因為獲利帶動成長，整體而言，新興市場企
業獲利表現強勁 ，而且仍持續加速成長 ，高盛預估2026年新興市場企業盈餘成長率將達 55%，超過標普500企業盈餘成長速
度的兩倍，且新興市場股票估值較美國股市存在超過40%的折價空間，評價相對具吸引力，今年以來流入新興市場股市的資金
已接近500億美元，創下過去20年來最強勁紀錄之一。更重要的是新興市場國家目前貢獻了全球近 80%的AI硬體出口。隨著AI
產業持續發展，受惠範圍有望從少數龍頭企業擴大至更多國家及公司，特別是那些能夠協助解決供應鏈瓶頸的企業。
• 結論：後市來看在AI產業趨勢、相對低估的評價水準，以及強勁企業獲利成長三大因素支撐下，新興市場股票未來一年仍具備

進一步上漲的潛力，有機會延續目前的強勢表現。

AI投資支撐美國成長，融資需求升高使長端利率與信用風險並存
• AI基礎建設持續擴張 ，成為企業投資重要動能 ：大型雲端業者
持續投入資料中心 、半導體、網路設備與電力基礎設施 ，帶動
相關產業資本支出與供應鏈需求 。摩根大通資產管理指出 ，納
入資料中心開發商及半導體投資後，AI 基礎建設至 2030 年的
累計投資需求可能達約 5兆美元 ，顯示AI投資循環仍具規模 ，
但實際經濟貢獻與企業獲利 ，仍取決於投資效率及商業化成果 。
• 外部融資需求增加 ，債券供給與投資回報成關鍵： 隨AI投資規
模擴大，大型科技企業逐步增加公司債與外部融資 ，在企業債
與公債供給同步增加下 ，長天期利率面臨上行壓力 。若未來AI
獲利成長未能跟上資本支出擴張速度 ，可能壓縮自由現金流與

資本報酬率，進一步擴大信用利差 ，並提高高估值科技股的評
價修正風險
• 市場觀察：AI基礎建設仍是未來數年推動全球科技投資與企業
數位轉型的成長主軸，相關需求具備中長期發展空間，而2026
年市場已逐漸轉向檢視投資回報率 、現金流創造能力及融資結
構是否具備可持續性 ，因此單純依賴資本支出擴張已不足以支
撐所有AI概念股評價 。建議配置可兼顧具獲利與現金流支撐的
AI供應鏈，以及高信用評等 、中短天期債券；對長天期債券則
須評估供給與利率風險 ，並審慎檢視高負債 AI企業的償債能力
及投資回報。
資料來源：Goldman Sachs，永豐金證券， 2026.10.12
AI投資持續挹注美國經濟，對名目GDP維持正面貢獻
資料來源：Morgan Stanley， Bloomberg，永豐金證券， 2026.10.12

AI熱潮、高債務與網路威脅，IMF示警三大金融穩定風險升高
資料來源：Bloomberg Finance L.P， Dealogic，Zero day Clock and IMF staff calculations.， 2026.10.13
AI相關風險持續升溫 主權債券殖利率攀升 網路資安威脅升高
大型雲端科技企業債券發行規模
（占投資等級公司債市場比重) 10年期公債殖利率平均變動幅度 零時差漏洞占比（%）
• AI估值高度集中，市場重定價風險升溫：IMF於10月《全球金融穩定報告》指出，AI相關投資已成為全球成長的重要支撐，
但資金與漲勢高度集中於少數企業及市場，一旦獲利成長不如預期或估值遭重新檢視，可能引發科技股修正並透過風險偏好
下降擴散至整體金融市場。
• 通膨與財政疑慮恐推升債券市場波動： IMF認為，若能源價格推升通膨壓力或市場對財政永續性產生疑慮 ，主權債殖利率可

能進一步上升，進而提高全球融資成本與資產重估風險；在高利率與高債務環境下，金融條件快速緊縮的情況將明顯提高。
• 網路安全風險成為潛在威脅： 隨金融體系數位化程度提高 ，IMF將大型網路攻擊列為重要風險來源之一 。若攻擊事件波及支
付系統、金融基礎設施或雲端服務平台，不僅可能影響市場運作效率，也可能透過信心衝擊與流動性壓力擴大金融市場波動。
• 投資建議：市場成長動能仍受AI支撐，但在金融穩定風險上升情境下，建議採取「AI受惠股＋高品質債券＋防禦型資產」的
槓鈴策略；核心配置可聚焦全球AI基礎建設供應鏈及投資等級債，藉由企業長期獲利成長與債券避險功能，同時因應估值修
正及金融條件收緊風險。

AI隱憂浮現但動能仍在
• CoreWeave信用風險亮紅燈：市場憂心雲端業者在算力租賃業務產生足夠回報前，恐難以負擔龐大債務，導致信用風險升
溫。上週，AI雲端業者CoreWeave的5年期信用違約交換（CDS）利差一度突破1,140個基點，升至通常反映嚴重信用壓力
的水準；旗下兩檔債券殖利率逼近15%，其中2032年到期的無擔保債券於上週三跌破面值的80%，相關賣壓並進一步蔓延
至股票市場。
• AI半導體產業的基本面仍具韌性：全球最大半導體設備商艾司摩爾（ASML）表示，客戶已開始洽談2029至2030年的訂單；
晶圓代工龍頭台積電財報亦優於市場預期。與此同時，台灣出口增速創3月以來新高，韓國9月半導體出口年增逾260%，顯
示半導體需求持續強勁，預期第三季相關企業獲利仍有望維持強勁。

美銀美林調查指出，全球經理人對景氣與股市仍維持偏多立場
資料來源：BofA Global Fund Manager Survey，2026.10
• 經理人樂觀情緒維持高檔： 美銀美林10月全球基金經理人調查顯示 ，市場風險偏好仍高 ，平均現金水位維持在 3.9%的低檔，
科技股配置則升至2020年8月以來最高，反映經理人對AI產業及企業獲利前景仍具信心 。然而，整體經濟成長預期已轉趨保
守，淨4%的受訪者預期未來 12個月經濟成長放緩 。極端風險方面，債券殖利率無序攀升仍居首位 ，占比31%；AI泡沫及通
膨再起分別為23%及20%。此外，39%的經理人認為，大型雲端業者資本支出是系統性信用事件的主要潛在來源 ，顯示市場
焦點已從AI需求成長延伸至龐大資本支出的獲利回收能力。

• 美國公債殖利率走高 ，債券的收益吸引力逐漸回升： 調查顯示，37%的受訪者表示，當美國10年期公債殖利率升至 6%時，
才會考慮由股票轉向債券；另有19%認為目前殖利率已具吸引力，顯示部分經理人已開始重新評估固定收益資產的配置價值 。
不過，債券整體仍處淨減碼，且經理人對科技股的配置持續升高 ，反映市場尚未全面轉向防禦。後續仍須關注美債殖利率變
化、AI資本支出的投資回報，以及企業獲利能否支撐偏高的市場評價。
資料來源：BofA Global Fund Manager Survey，2026.10
基金經理人現金水位 最可能影響市場表現的極端風險

台積電財報佳卻未激勵股價，短線面臨總經干擾
• 台積電Q3營收、獲利及EPS同步改寫單季新高：營收年增50.9%、季增17.6%，稅後純益7,630.6億元，年增68.7％、季增8
％；每股稅後純益(EPS)29.43元，整體優於市場預期，但毛利率66.4％較第二季的67.7％下降。
• 財測展望：第四季財測營收預估 523~543億美元，季增上看15％、年增65％，毛利率預估65%~67%，反映高階晶片需求
依然強勁。全年展望方面，預期2026年美元營收年增約44%，較上季預估的「略高於40%」說法更加明確。此外，重申全
年資本支出600~640億美元不變。
• 市場反應：台積電法說會隔日股價小幅下跌，法說會未出現激勵行情，主要原因包含美國通膨升溫壓力、外資連續調節部位，

以及毛利率略為下降。短線籌碼面可能仍會調整，但台積電董事長魏哲家在法說會中強調，第二波AI需求正強勁興起，台積
電正透過多種途徑全力擴充產能，顯示高階晶片前景明確，中長期具投資價值，多家投資機構也紛紛上調目標價。
資料來源：台積電法說會，兆豐銀行整理，2026.10.15。
法說會重點整理 說明
AI需求持續升溫
魏哲家坦言，目前仍難以判斷晶圓供需缺口將擴大或縮小，因為
客戶需求持續增加，台積電也正積極提高產能。儘管消費性電子
市場仍受到零組件漲價及總體經濟不確定性影響，但先進製程需
求依然強勁，公司預估2026年全年美元營收將年增約44％。
擴廠進度
(1)台南科學園區新建3奈米廠預計2027年下半年量產，並決定在
台南再增建一座3奈米廠，規畫2028年投入量產。
(2)美國亞利桑那州第二座晶圓廠已完成廠房興建，預計2027年
第三季量產，時程較原先規畫略為提前；日本第二座晶圓廠則將
導入3奈米製程，預計2028年量產。
台積電證實洽談Terafab
合作
魏哲家強調，目前相關合作仍在討論階段，尚未有結論。不過，

無論未來採取何種合作模式，晶圓廠都必須由台積電負責營運。
電力供應疑慮
美國電力供應情況是公司評估AI長期需求的重要因素之一，因此
台積電進一步確認當地電力供應狀況，目前相關研究及評估結果
均良好，尚未發現明顯問題。

• 全球經濟正處於「韌性仍在、風險升高」的階段：國際貨幣基金組織(IMF)指出，AI投資熱潮持續推動美國、台灣等國家成
長，但中東地緣政治衝突、高通膨風險、政府債務問題與AI泡沫化疑慮，也同時為市場埋下不確定因素。
• 市場永遠充滿雜音，定期定額紀律投資更具優勢：本許多人認為定期定額的核心在於「平均成本」，但更重要的是幫助投資
人克服人性。市場上漲時持續投入，可以參與企業獲利成長帶來的成果，市場下跌時持續扣款，則能以較低價格累積更多單
位數。換句話說，定期定額讓投資人不需要每天判斷：明天會不會跌？現在是不是太貴？要不要先停扣觀望？長期而言，紀

律投資往往比短期擇時的成效更佳。
• AI時代更適合定期定額：儘管IMF認為人工智慧發展為全球經濟成長帶來貢獻，但同時也警告，若市場對AI未來獲利能力過
度樂觀，不排除出現股市估值修正。根據歷史經驗，每一次市場重大修正往往也孕育下一輪成長契機，不論AI發展是否出現
泡沫，善用定期定額策略可以在機會與風險之間達到平衡。
韌性仍在、風險升溫，紀律投資更穩健

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年7-9月期GDP予測（1次速報） ～前期比年率＋0.4％と予想。成長率は鈍化も、景気回復の流れは崩れず～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

Economic Indicators

2026 年 10 月30 日

1/3 
2026 年 7-9 月期 GDP 予測（1 次速報） 
～前期比年率＋0.4％と予想。成長率は鈍化も、景気回復の流れは崩れず～ 
経済調査部 シニアエグゼクティブエコノミスト 新家義貴 
（℡：050-5474-7490）

小幅ながら４四半期連続のプラス成長を予想、景気回復基調は維持 
11 月16 日に公表される2026 年７-９月期の実質ＧＤＰ成長率を前期比年率＋0.4％（前期比＋
0.1％）と予測する。４四半期連続のプラス成長になるだろう1。 
物価高による下押しや耐久財需要の反動に加え、天候不順の影響もあって個人消費が減少する
一方、設備投資が高い伸びになることが下支えになる見込みである。設備投資の伸びには特殊要
因も一部含まれているが、それを除いても堅調だったと考えられる。個人消費が弱含むなかでも
プラス成長を維持するとみられることは、企業部門の底堅さが景気を下支えしていることを示し
ている。過去３四半期に比較的高い成長が続いた後であることも踏まえれば、今回の減速をもっ
て景気の基調が弱まったと判断する必要はないだろう。家計部門の弱さは懸念材料だが、企業部
門の底堅さは維持されており、景気は引き続き緩やかな回復基調にあると評価できる。

輸入増による成長率押し下げと公的在庫のプラス寄与は表裏一体 
７-９月期のＧＤＰを評価する際には、原油輸入と公的在庫の動きをセットで捉える必要があ
る。４-６月期は、中東情勢の悪化によって原油輸入が激減したことが、ＧＤＰ統計上は輸入減を
通じて成長率を押し上げた。一方、輸入の減少による国内供給の不足を補うために国家備蓄原油
が放出され、公的在庫の取り崩しが進んだ。この結果、公的在庫変動は前期比寄与度▲0.5％Pt
（前期比年率寄与度▲2.1％Pt）と、成長率を大きく押し下げた。輸入減による成長率の押し上げ
は、公的在庫の減少と表裏一体の関係にあった。 
７-９月期は、これと逆の動きが生じるとみられる。政府・民間による代替調達の進展によって
原油輸入が回復するため、統計上は輸入増が成長率の押し下げ要因になる。一方、輸入の回復に
伴って国家備蓄原油の取り崩しは一服しており、公的在庫の減少幅は前期に比べて大きく縮小す
るとみられる。この結果、公的在庫変動は前期比寄与度＋0.4％Pt（前期比年率寄与度＋1.5％
Pt）と、成長率を大きく押し上げることが予想される。

1 10 月 30 日時点で入手可能な経済指標を元に作成している。国際収支や家計調査等、今後公表される経済指標の結果
を踏まえて予測値を修正する可能性がある。

### Page 2

2/3 
輸入増による成長率の押し下げと公的在庫変動による押し上げは、ともに原油の供給環境が改
善したことに伴う動きである。両者を個別に景気の強弱として評価することは適切ではなく、一
体として見るべきだろう。 
むしろ重要なのは、代替調達の進展によって国家備蓄への依存を減らしながら必要な原油を確
保できる状況になり、資源不足が生産活動を大きく制約するリスクが低下していることである。
ＧＤＰ統計上、輸入の増加は成長率の押し下げ要因となるものの、経済実態としては国内で利用
可能な資源の供給回復を意味している。この点は、７-９月期の日本経済を評価するうえで、むし
ろ前向きに捉えるべきだろう。

需要項目別の動向 
個人消費は前期比▲0.2％と小幅減少を予想する。８～９月に各地で相次いだ大雨や台風などの
天候不順が外出機会やサービス消費を抑制した可能性がある。また、４-６月期には自動車やエア
コンなどの耐久財販売が増加したが、７-９月期にはこうした一時的な押し上げ効果が剥落した。
加えて、物価高による節約志向が根強いなか、食料品や日用品などの非耐久財消費にも弱さがみ
られる。高い賃上げや底堅い雇用環境は下支え要因となっているものの、天候不順という一時的
な要因もあって個人消費は総じて力強さを欠く結果になったとみられる。 
設備投資は前期比＋2.0％と、高い伸びを予想する。４-６月期には特許権の海外譲渡に伴う研
究開発投資の減少という特殊要因があったが、７-９月期はその影響が剥落することが押し上げ要
因になった面はある。もっとも、こうした特殊要因を除いても設備投資は明確に増加したと考え
られ、底堅い動きとなっている。企業収益が高水準を維持するなか、人手不足に対応した省力化
投資やデジタル・ＡＩ関連投資への需要は根強く、企業の設備投資意欲は引き続き強い。設備投
資計画も高い伸びとなっており、設備投資は均してみれば緩やかな増加基調にあると判断してよ
いだろう。 
なお、特許権の海外への譲渡により、テクニカルに４-６月期はサービス輸出が上振れ、設備投
資が下振れたが、７-９月期はその剥落によりサービス輸出が下振れ、設備投資が上振れとなる見
込みである。両者は相殺し合うため、成長率全体でみれば概ねニュートラルとなる。 
輸出は前期比＋1.2％と４四半期連続の増加を予想する。４-６月期に大口要因によって研究開
発サービスの受取が急増した分が剥落することで、サービス輸出が減少する一方、財輸出が高い
伸びとなり、財貨・サービス輸出全体では増加を維持したとみられる。半導体製造装置などの資
本財輸出が好調だった。地域別では、ＥＵ向けが低調だった一方、ＡＳＥＡＮ、ＮＩＥｓ向けの
資本財輸出が伸びている。また、４-６月期に急減した中東向け輸出が持ち直したことも押し上げ
につながっている。 
輸入は前期比＋3.3％と大幅な増加が予想される。４-６月期には資源輸入の激減により輸入が
大幅に減少していたが、代替調達の進展により７-９月期は明確に持ち直した。この結果、外需寄
与度は前期比▲0.3％Pt（前期比年率▲1.3％Pt）になったと予想される。 
ただし、この外需寄与度のマイナスは、資源輸入の回復に伴う輸入の増加によって生じるもの

PC Semis: More divergent
outlooks
Business diversification, KGD (known good die) pricing power
and supply chain management create a divergent revenue
outlook for PC semis into 4Q and likely also 1Q27. Downgrade
Elan to EW.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Daniel Yen, CFA
Equity Analyst
Daniel.Yen@morganstanley.com                                                +886 2 2730-2863
Charlie Chan
Equity Analyst

Morgan Stanley appreciates your support in
the 2027 Extel Asia Research Survey. Voting
opens 10 November 2026.
Greater China Technology Semiconductors
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive
What’s Changed
Elan Microelectronics Corp
(2458.TW) From To
Rating Overweight Equal-weight
Price Target NT$250.00 NT$160.00
A much tougher 4Q… We mentioned in the prior PC semis note that the FOMP
(fear of missing procurement) could lead to  stable PC semis shipments in 3Q; now,

the inventory accumulation in 1Q-3Q26 could cause a deeper correction in 4Q.  PC
semis customers currently still aim for only a 5-10% Q/Q procurement decline in 4Q.
That said, the downside surprise in Parade’s 4Q outlook (link), which calls for a 20-
25% Q/Q revenue decline, could suggest a worse component correction. The still-
elevated Windows activation period of 200+ days and much smaller price hikes
from PC semis customers in 2H vs. 1H could suggest ongoing weakness into 1Q27.

But why are some companies resilient? Many investors are asking why Realtek’s
revenue could be up Q/Q in 4Q amid the PC correction (link). We cite three factors:
1) Consumer demand (other than PC/smartphones) is not necessarily all bad,
especially for IoT devices (low memory content). Espressif is guiding for  Q/Q
revenue growth in 4Q, following sequential growth in 3Q. 2) Price hikes reflect
increases at both  foundries/OSATs and in KGD. For example, TV SoC pricing is

several times higher than in past years due to  KGD price hikes (mainly DDR4). 3)
Large-scale IC design houses usually have better supply chain management and
lower exposure to PC.
No new growth driver into 2027: With the recent debate on cloud AI, we think it’s
still a matter of time before edge AI  takes off. However, we haven’t seen a clear
growth driver for PC semis. Preliminary outlooks from PC semis customers range
from flattish to down 20% Y/Y in 2027. Thus, it's  difficult to turn positive now.

Reduce earnings: We revise down Elan's PT and earnings and cut the stock to EW.
We appreciate the company's growth from smart Edge AI devices, but its exposure
to NB is still too significant to overlook. Our PT implies 16x our downward-revised
2027 EPS. Stay EW  Realtek and UW  Novatek, ASMedia, Parade, and Nuvoton.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
The FOMP (fear of missing procurement for PC semis in 1Q-3Q26) has caused a
deeper correction for some PC semis into 4Q.
It's easier for large-scale PC semis  to navigate through the downturn, while small
design houses are more concentrated on PC.
We stay conservative on PC semis, with no OW and await the cycle bottom

sometime in 2027.
November 3, 2026 09:00 PM GMT

Elan Micro: Earnings estimate revisions
We revise our EPS estimates by +1%/-20%/-16% for 2026/27/28: For 2026, we
incorporate actual 2Q26 results, which benefited from investment gains. For 2027-28, we
reduce our revenue and GM forecasts due to continued weak PC demand, which we
believe cannot be fully offset by the non PC business (mainly the drone market).
Exhibit 1: Elan: Estimate revisions
Source: Company data, Morgan Stanley Research (e) estimates.

Exhibit 2: Elan: Quarterly financial statement
Source: Company data, Morgan Stanley Research (e) estimates
Valuation Methodology
We cut our price target to NT$160: This reflects our earnings estimate revisions for
2026-28. Our price target is our base-case scenario value, which we continue to derive
from our residual income model. We reduce our intermediate growth rate estimate from
10.0% to 8.0% to reflect a weak PC business outlook. We reduce our terminal growth rate

estimate from 4.5% to 3.0% to better align it with broader economic growth. We continue
to assume a cost of equity of 9.6% and a payout ratio of 80%.
Our bull case value is cut to NT$234 from NT$366, and our bear case value is cut to NT
$84 from NT$131.

Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 3

Chroma ATE (2360.TW)
05 November 2026 Citi Research
3
Chroma ATE
Valuation
Our target price of NT$2,900 is based on 40x 2027E P/E, at close to 1x PEG. Chroma shares have re-rated above its historical
range as its growth outlook is now strongly tied to AI infrastructure build out. We estimate 60%+ of its revenue are now AI-
related, with strong growth across product segments including power testers, SLT, metrology, and photonics. Chroma has a
commanding market share in most of these segments and well positioned to capitalized on the global AI expansion.

Risks
Downside risks to achieving our target price include: 1) softer AI/CPO growth expectation due to changes in capex outlook or
production bottleneck; 2) fewer SLT needed as a result of shortened cycle time as GPU yield rate improves; 3) disappointing
metrology shipment due to changes at ramp-up schedule; 4) testing process change leading to loss in market share; 5) FX risks
(50-60% of Chroma’s sales are in US$, 10-20% in Rmb).
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

### Page 6

WinWay Technology (6515.TW)
06 November 2026 Citi Research
6
WinWay Technology
Company description
WinWay, founded in 2001, is a global leader in semiconductor test interfaces
and the world's largest test socket supplier. Originally specializing in high-
speed solutions for IC design and OSAT firms, WinWay now dominates the AI
and HPC sectors. The company provides a comprehensive portfolio including
coaxial sockets, burn-in sockets, probe cards (MEMS & VPC), and advanced

thermal management systems. Its flagship hypersocket technology and
vertically integrated manufacturing enable high-performance validation for
next-generation accelerators. WinWay's diverse solutions support mission-
critical applications across global AI infrastructure, networking, and high-
speed computing.
Investment strategy
We rate WinWay at Buy/High Risk (1H). We believe WinWay is well positioned
for the AI infrastructure cycle, with dominance in the US AI GPU testing

supply chain and participation in US CSP AI ASIC projects. Its leading
proprietary coaxial socket and hypersocket technologies should provide a
defensible technical moat as 224G SerDes and extreme package complexities
expand. The company captures a wider share of the test interface market
through its premium sockets and high-quality service, providing a multi-
layered earnings engine supported by structural growth in AI testing intensity.
Valuation

We value WinWay using a PE-based approach that factors in earnings
momentum and likely expansion of its addressable market. Our target price of
NT$6,500 is derived from applying c.30x to 2H27E-1H28E EPS, in line with
its historical average PE since 2023, which we believe is justified given the
company's entrenched position in the high-end AI GPU testing supply chain,
rising contribution from coaxial socket and SLT solutions, and the increasing
value of test content per module. With these structural drivers in place, and

as we believe the market is starting to value it as an AI/HPC play (rather than a
consumer electronics stock), we expect the multiple to hold at a premium
relative to its historical averages.
Risks
Our quant rating system rates the WinWay stock High Risk based on its
historical volatility.
Key downside risks that could impede the stock from reaching our target price
and our earnings forecasts include: 1) client and sector concentration –
specifically any delays in US AI GPU product roadmap could impact the

business outlook for WinWay; 2) intensifying competition in coaxial and
hypersockets from global peers; 3) faster-than-expected testing algorithm
optimization, which could shorten testing durations and reduce the
anticipated hardware demand multiplier; and 4) delay of MEMS Phase 2 mass
production.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: Following our results memo on Vanguard International (VIS), 
we raise our forecasts and 12M TP and upgrade our rating one notch to 
Buy (1), given a widened disconnect between strengthened fundamentals 
yet weakened valuations. Assessed in our Asia Foundry report, VIS’s wafer 
loadings should approach full through 2027, thanks to demand spillover 
from AI scaling augmented by TSMC’s fab optimisation, with better ASP 
adding to growth. While the equity markets appear to be haunted by the US 
mid-term elections, management sees it as a “healthy wakeup call” to 
anyone with overheated AI expectations; we concur.

What’s the impact: Forecast revisions. Since operating metrics at VIS 
look broadly intact except for higher ASP as some customers’ previous 
long-term agreements expired later, we only finetune our forecasts by 
increasing our 2026-28E EPS 2-6%. We stick with our view that VIS will 
enjoy counter-seasonal prints through 2027 with sustained strong earnings 
growth, thanks again to a paradigm shift we’ve observed in the specialty 
foundry market benefiting VIS, aided by its fast 12” capacity ramp.

A healthy wake-up call? We were impressed by management’s 
commentary about potential impact from the US mid-term elections that it 
should cool down any overheated expectations for the AI buildouts in the 
markets, since deployments among AI factories, power infrastructure, 
environment and social welfare need to be reviewed and optimised for a 
right balance. That said, management views this as a healthy wake-up call 
to the markets since AI has walked in our daily lives, becoming a 
fundamental demand for an unreversed secular trend. We believe its 
commentary supports our view calling for likely a soft-landing 2028 when 
hyperscalers’ investments slow down and new supply catches up to help 
discover inflated demand, fulfil unmet demand and manage excess 
inventory for a healthy growth and balance between AI and non-AI 
consumer ecosystems. Though the correction could be painful, the equity 
markets appear to have discounted this risk now than later, which looks to 
set a healthy wakeup call to the equity market as well, in our view.

What we recommend: Despite our forecast revisions, we trim our 12M TP 
for VIS to TWD200 (previous: TWD210), on our lower ROE-adjusted target 
PBR of 5x (previous: 5.2x) pegging to our lowered target multiple for TSMC 
(2330 TT, TWD2,235, Buy [1]) That said, we upgrade our rating one notch 
to Buy (1) on valuation grounds with key downside risks of the current chip 
upturn ending earlier and 12” ramp/execution coming in below expectation.

How we differ: Our 2026-27E EPS are 3-11% above the consensus 
forecasts likely due to our higher trajectory of ASP assumptions.

9 November 2026

Upgrading: a healthy reset in expectations

➢ Fine-tuning up our forecasts after the results call 
➢ A healthy wakeup call from the US mid-term elections? 
➢ Upgrading rating to Buy (1) with a revised 12M TP of TWD200

Company profile 
Founded in December 1994 with stocks listed in March 1998, Vanguard International Semiconductor 
Corp. (VIS) successfully transformed its business model from memory to complementary metal oxide 
semiconductor (CMOS) logic foundry focusing on specialty technologies such as mixed signal, power 
analog and embedded memory, growing as one leading specialty foundry in the world. Leveraging a 
strategic relationship with its major shareholder TSMC, VIS operates wafer fabs in Taiwan and 
Singapore, servicing a high variety of customers including fabless chipmakers and IDMs around the 
world.

5

Vanguard International Semiconductor (5347 TT): 9 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 9 Oct 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 1
VIS's board of directors (BoD) consists of 9 members, including 5 independent directors,
representing 56% of the total board seats. The board comprises members with diverse
backgrounds in business management, finance, industry, and academia, providing a broad
range of expertise and perspectives.
We believe the majority-independent board structure and the directors’ collective experience in
leadership, corporate governance, industry expertise, financial management, risk management,
and sustainability strengthen the effectiveness of the BoD and support VIS's long-term growth
and corporate governance practices.
Capital management 1 VIS pays its dividend on a quarterly basis. In the past 5 years, it has managed its dividend payout well, as evidenced by its dividend in dollar terms, where it has never lowered its dividend.
Related party &
transaction 1 Sales to related parties were around 1-2% of its total revenue each year. The percentage is insignificant and thus we see limited risk for VIS.
S Supply chain management 1
VIS has established a comprehensive sustainable supply chain management framework to
ensure suppliers comply with its sustainability requirements covering environmental protection,
labor and human rights, occupational health and safety, business ethics, and responsible
sourcing. The company requires suppliers to adhere to its "VIS Supplier Code of Conduct" and
"Corporate Sustainability Policy", and conducts supplier sustainability assessments and audits
as part of its supplier management process. We believe these efforts strengthen VIS's supply
chain resilience and help mitigate supply chain risks.
E Water & wastewater management 2
VIS is committed to improving water efficiency and sustainable water management. In 2022, the
company implemented the ISO 46001 Water Efficiency Management System to analyze water
consumption patterns, identify significant water-use equipment, evaluate water-saving
opportunities, and establish improvement measures. VIS also provides annual water
management training at its Taiwan facilities to promote continuous improvement in water-use
efficiency. We believe these efforts demonstrate the company's commitment to responsible
water stewardship and help reduce operational risks associated with water resources.
E
Waste & hazardous
materials
management
1
VIS adopts a comprehensive resource management approach to waste management and has
established waste management procedures in accordance with ISO 14001 standards. Through
dedicated waste management personnel and regular employee training, the company promotes
effective waste reduction, classification, collection, storage, and treatment practices across its
operations.
E GHG emissions 1
VIS adopts a source-segregation and multi-stage treatment approach, supported by high-
efficiency air pollution control systems, to effectively manage VOC and acidic/alkaline gas
emissions from semiconductor manufacturing processes.
The company's emission levels remain well below regulatory limits, reflecting its commitment to
environmental compliance and sustainable operations.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

營收走勢圖

研究員/鄭高輯 OWENCHENG@uni-psg.com

結論: 
環球晶短期獲利表現低於預期，主要原因包括: (1) 義大利 Novara
廠 7 月火災影響、(2)短期產能受限，以及(3)2H26 仍適用舊LTA 價
格 。 不過最壞狀況已過 ， 隨著時序進入4Q26， 預期營收將優於3Q26，
且自 1Q27 起 ， 隨著新產能開出 ， 以及開始適用價格調漲後的新LTA，
獲利表現將明顯轉佳 。 預估2026、2027 年稅後 EPS 分別為17.82 元
(原估 19.49 元)、33.14 元(原估 32.53 元)。在投資建議上，儘管
短期營運不如預期 ， 但環球晶股價已大幅修正 ， 且2027 年半導體及
記憶體新 Fab 產能開出的趨勢明確(13 座->23 座)， 因此矽晶圓供需
轉緊且價格上漲的趨勢不變， 故維持買進建議 ， 然考量近期AI 供應
鏈出貨不順的雜音較多 ，漲價概念股評價受壓抑 ， 故將PBR 由 6X 調
整至 5X (PBR 歷史區間 2~6X，漲價循環合理評價應在中上緣 4~6X
之間)，將目標價由 1350 元下調至 1170 元。

重點摘要:

1.3Q26 財報表現不佳，主要受義大利 Novara 廠火災影響:  
3Q26 營收-2.3%QoQ(+2.6%YoY)，若排除 7 月義大利 Novara 廠火災
之影響，營收原本可以成長雙位數%QoQ。在毛利率方面，儘管3Q26
折舊費用略較前季下降 ， 但因Novara 火災導致部分高毛利率的 8 吋
SOI 基板出貨中斷 ， 導致毛利率較前季下滑0.6pt 至 20%， 不過在費
用控管下，OPM 較前季回升 0.4pt 至 9.8%。單季稅後 EPS 3.13 元，
低於原預期的 3.64 元 ， 且明顯低於前季的7.9 元 ， 差異主要係2Q26
認列較多 WAF 金融評價利益。

2.美光支付 5 億美元並非是預付款，而是認購 CB:  
今年 7 月環球晶與美光宣布簽訂 10 年期 LTA，美光將支付環球晶 5
億美元，然而，並非以預付款形式支付，而是環球晶發行上限 5 億
美元的可轉債，由美光擔任唯一應募人。研究員認為，客戶支付預
付款綁定產能的強制力相對較強 (預付款在出貨後會轉營收且不退
還)，然而，CB 存在股本稀釋的問題(推估約 3%上下)，相對較不受
歡迎。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

3.2027 年起開始適用新 LTA 價格，ASP 將改善:  
在價格趨勢方面，公司對於價格調整將採取逐案談判 (Case-by-
case)，依據產品規格、供需狀況、合約到期時間與個別客戶情況進
行協議，非全產品線統一調漲特定百分比。公司認為目前市場環境
日益支持價格回歸合理水準，且已有多筆新 LTA 接近洽談完成，預
計自 2027 年起整體 ASP 將受惠於新 LTA 與產品組合優化而有所改
善。

3.4Q26 營收會優於 3Q26，但受限產能，且價格仍沿用舊合約:  
展望 4Q26，義大利 Novara 廠已正式啟動部分復工，且整體客戶需
求動能依然非常強勁 ， 甚至有客戶願意提前適用新價格以爭取產能 ，
但受限於產能不足 ， 且4Q26 絕大多數價格仍適用舊合約 ， 因此預期
4Q26 營收會優於 3Q26， 但仍無法達成雙位數%成長 。 不過自1Q27 起，
隨著新產能開出以及 ASP 提升，營運動能將轉佳。研究員預估2026
年營收-1.0%YoY，稅後EPS 17.82 元(原估 19.49 元，下修主要是因
為火災)。

4.2027 年營運將明顯好轉，大致維持獲利預估:  
展望 2027 年 ， 根據研調統計 ， 開始營運投片的新Fab 數量將自 2026
年的 13 座大幅增加至 2027 年的 23 座 ， 且成熟製程庫存健康化 ，電
源管理、車用與工業恢復，公司預期 2027 年矽晶圓供需環境將比
2026 年更吃緊。就環球晶而言，自 1Q27 起隨著新產能開出，且開
始適用調漲後的新 LTA 價格，將有助於營收及獲利轉強。此外，隨
著 AI 資料中心發展 ，SiC 市場正步入新成長週期 ，12 吋 SOI 晶圓也
受惠矽光子強勁動能推升需求，密蘇里州廠是目前美國唯一具備12
吋 SOI 製造能力的基地，產能持續爬升，客戶意向及預付款已可涵
蓋擬新增產能的 90%以上。基於以上，環球晶將步入產業上升循環，
預估 2027 年營收+33.8%YoY，稅後 EPS 33.14 元(原估 32.53 元)。

2026 年 11 月 2 日 全6 頁

7-9 月期ユーロ圏 GDP 減速しつつも上振れ 
実質 GDP 成長率は前期比＋0.4％と市場予想を上回る

ロンドンリサーチセンター シニアエコノミスト 橋本 政彦

[要約] 
 ユーロ圏の2026 年7-9 月期の実質GDP 成長率（速報値）は前期比＋0.4％と、4-6 月期
（同＋0.6％）から成長ペースが鈍化しつつも、市場予想（Bloomberg 調査：同＋0.2％）
を上回る底堅い結果であった。 
 ユーロ圏の成長率の半分は、多国籍大 企業の影響を受けたアイルランドの高成長によ
るものである点は割り引いて見る必要がある。 ただし、アイルランドを除いてもプラ
ス成長を維持しており、 エネルギー高を背景としたインフレ率の加速や、記録的猛暑
による悪影響という逆風にもかかわらず、ユーロ圏経済は底堅く推移したという評価
に変わりはない。 
 国別の内訳では、スペインが内需の堅調さを主因として前期比＋0.6％と高成長を維持
し、ユーロ圏全体をけん引する構図が続いた。また、ドイツは同＋0.2％と、前期から
成長率は鈍化したものの、市場予想（同＋0.1％）を上回った。他方、フランスは同▲
0.1％と 2 四半期ぶりのマイナス成長に転じる低調な結果であった。 
 ユーロ圏経済は 10-12 月期も緩やかな景気拡大が続く見通しである。エネルギー価格
の上昇によるインフレ率の加速が個人消費を下振れさせるリスクには注意が必要だが、
ドイツを中心とした拡張財政がユーロ圏経済を下支えすると見込まれる。

### Page 2

1

2 / 6

成長の半分はアイルランドの上振れ、ただしそれを割り引いても底堅い 
ユーロ圏の 2026 年 7-9 月期の実質 GDP 成長率（速報値）は前期比＋0.4％と、4-6 月期（同
＋0.6％）から成長ペースが鈍化しつつも、市場予想（Bloomberg 調査、以下同：同＋0.2％）
を上回る底堅い結果であった。 
ただし、7-9 月期の上振れには、毎期の振れが非常に大きいアイルランドの高成長が影響し
ている点は割り引いて見る必要がある。 アイルランドの成長率は前期比 ＋5.5％と、成長率が
公表された 12 ヵ国の中で突出して高く、これがユーロ圏全体の成長 のおよそ半分を占めてい
る。CSO（アイルランド中央統計局 ）は、多国籍企業の影響が大きいセクターが押し上げ要因
になったと報告しており、一部の多国籍大企業の企業内取引などが成長率を押し上げたとみら
れる。アイルランドを除くユーロ圏 20 ヵ国の GDP 成長率（大和総研試算）は同＋0.2％となり、
実態としての景気回復ペースはヘッドラインよりも緩やかだったとみるべきだろう。もっとも、
アイルランドを除いてもプラス成長を維持しており、エネルギー高を背景としたインフレ率の
加速や、記録的猛暑による悪影響という逆風にもかかわらず、ユーロ圏経済は底堅く推移した
という評価に変わりはない。 
国ごとの成長率を確認 すると、スペイン、オランダ、エストニアがいずれも前期比＋ 0.6％
となり、既述のアイルランドの次に成長率が高かった。スペインの成長率は前期（同＋ 0.7％）
から減速したが、ユーロ圏全体に対する寄与度はアイルランドに次いで大きく、スペインの高
成長がユーロ圏全体をけん引する構図は7-9 月期も続いた。また、ドイツは同＋0.2％と、こち
らも前期（同＋0.3％）から成長ペースが鈍化しつつも、市場予想（同＋0.1％）を上回り、ユ
ーロ圏全体の成長を下支えした。他方、不調だったのがフランスであり、増加を見込んでいた
市場予想（同＋0.1％）に反して、同▲0.1％と2 四半期ぶりのマイナス成長に転じている。

イルランド）を差し引いて計算。

フランスは輸出・投資が減少、スペインは引き続き内需主導の成長維持 
ユーロ圏全体の 7-9 月期の需要項目別の内訳は速報段階で明らかになっていないため、以下
では内訳が明らかになっているフランスとスペインの需要項目別の動きを見ていく。 
フランスの実質 GDP 成長率は前期比▲0.1％と、2 四半期ぶりのマイナス成長となった。内・
外需別では、内需の前期比寄与度は＋0.5％pt とプラスだった一方、外需寄与度が同▲0.6％pt
となり GDP を押し下げた。内需の内訳では、在庫変動が最大の押し上げ要因（前期比寄与度：
＋0.4％pt）となった。ただし、内需から在庫を除いた国内最終需要も 前期からわずかに増加
ペースが加速しており、GDP 全体としてはマイナス成長ながら、内需については一定の底堅さ
が見られたと評価できる。 
需要項目別の詳細を見ていくと、個人消費は前期比 ＋0.4％と前期から加速し、これが内需
を押し上げた。増加幅は2024 年7-9 月期以来の大きさであり、個人消費に限れば7-9 月期はむ
しろ好調だった と評価できる。内訳 では、財（同＋0.4％） 、サービス（同＋0.4％）がいずれ
も前期から加速しており、 個人消費は幅広く増加し ている。財消費では、エネルギーが同＋
1.2％と増加に転じたことが主な押し上げ 要因となった 。ガソリン価格 が上昇する中、石油精
製品の減少は続いたものの、電力消費の増加がエネルギー全体を押し上げた。また、エネルギ
ー以外の財では 、電気自動車の販売増加により、輸送用機器が同＋3.1 ％と好調だった。サー
ビス消費では、娯楽関連を中心とした家庭向けサービスの増加（同＋0.9％）、および宿泊・飲
食サービス（同＋0.7％）が主な押し上げ要因となった。 
好調だった個人消費とは対照的に、総固定資本形成 は前期比▲0.6％と 3 四半期連続で減少
し、内需の足を引っ張った 。最大の押し下げ要因となったのは、 輸送用機器が同▲7.0％と大
幅に減少したことである。統計公表元の INSEE（フランス国立統計経済研究所）の報告によれ
ば、前期にあったクルーズ船の受け渡しの反動、および企業による投資の減少が押し下げ 要因
となった。このほか、建設投資が同▲0.5％と3 四半期連続で減少したほか、不動産取引の停滞
により、法律サービスなどのビジネスサービス（同▲ 1.6％ ）、不動産サービス （同▲0.7％）
が前期から減少した。他方、IT 機器の増加により資本財が同＋0.8％と増加したほか、サービ
ス投資の中でも情報通信サービスは同＋2.1％と増加した。 
外需に関して、輸出は前期比▲1.7％と2 四半期ぶりに減少した。前期に大幅に増加した航空
関連が反動で 同▲9.1 ％と大きく減少したことが減少の主因と なった。 また、 農産品 （同▲
7.0％）、農作物加工品（同▲1.1％）が減少したほか、資本財が同▲1.1％と 7 四半期ぶりの減
少に転じるなど 、財輸出が総じて振るわなかった。 サービス輸出は同＋0.5％と増加を維持し
たが、増加幅は前期（同＋1.0％）から減速している。 
輸入は前期比＋0.1％と、小幅ながら増加した。自動車、および海軍装備品の増加によって
輸送用機器が同＋0.3％増加したことや、資本財の増加（同＋1.4％）が押し上げに寄与した。
他方、石油精製品（同▲6.4 ％ ）、農作物加工品（同▲1.6％）が減少したことで 、輸入全体の
増加幅は小幅にとどまった。

### Page 4

1

4 / 6

図表 2 フランス・スペインの実質 GDP 成長率と需要項目別寄与度

（出所）INSEE、INE、Haver Analytics より大和総研作成

スペインの実質 GDP 成長率は前期比＋0.6％となり、4-6 月期（同＋0.7％）から成長ペース
が減速しつつも、高い成長率を維持した。内 ・外需別では、内需 の前期比寄与度が ＋0.5％pt、
外需が同＋0.1％pt となり、引き続き堅調な内需が成長のドライバーとなっている。 
個人消費は前期比＋0.6％となった。形態別の内訳では、サービスが同＋0.9％と、15 四半期
連続で増加し好調さを維持したことに加え、耐久財が同＋0.5％と2 四半期連続で増加し、非耐
久財は同＋0.6％と 4 四半期ぶりの増加に転じている。他方、半耐久財は同▲2.2％と減少に転
じた。 
総固定資本形成は前期比＋0.7 ％と、11 四半期連続 の増加を維持した。ただし、増加幅は
2025 年 4-6 月期以来の小ささとなり、増加ペースは鈍化している。建設投資が同＋1.0％と堅
調さを維持する一方で、輸送用機械の減少が下押し要因となり、機械・設備・武器システムは
同＋0.2％と小幅な増加にとどまった。 
外需に関して、輸出は前期比＋1.3％と4 四半期連続で増加し、増加幅が前期から拡大した。
インバウンド（非居住者による支出）の増加（ 同＋1.6％）を中心に、サービス 輸出が同＋
1.5％と増加し、輸出全体をけん引する状況が続いている が、サービス輸出 の伸びは前期から
縮小した。他方、財輸出は同＋1.1％と、増加幅はサービス輸出に比べて小さいものの、前期
から増加ペースが加速している。 
輸入は前期比＋1.1％となった。サービス輸入が同＋2.1％と大きく増加したことに加え、財
輸入も同＋0.8％と増加した。輸入の増加幅は前期から縮小し、輸出の増加幅を下回ったため、
外需（純輸出）の寄与度は2 四半期ぶりのプラスに転じている。 
なお、ドイツ （前期比＋0.2 ％）については、まだ内訳の詳細な計数は公表されていないが、

### Page 5

1

5 / 6

ドイツ連邦統計局からは、消費支出の増加が押し上げに寄与したこと、一方で総固定資本形成
が減少したことが報告されている。また、輸出については好調だった2026 年上期から減速した
とコメントされた。イタリア（同横ばい）については、内需寄与度のプラスと、外需寄与度の
マイナスがイタリア国家統計局によって報告されている。これらの情報を総合すると、7-9 月
期は、個人消費を中心とした内需がユーロ圏の成長に寄与した可能性が高い。

防衛・インフラ投資が景気拡大を下支え、リスクはエネルギー価格 
ユーロ圏経済の 10-12 月期に入ってからの景気動向に関して、欧州委員会が公表するユーロ
圏景況感指数（総合）を確認すると、2026 年10 月は前月差▲0.6pt と2 ヵ月連続で低下した。
もっとも、過去の関係に照らせば10 月の指数の水準は、景気悪化を示すほどの低さではなく、
ユーロ圏経済は引き続き緩やかなペースでの拡大が続いていることを示唆する。 
景況感指数の内訳を見ると、消費者信頼感指数が前月差▲1.1pt と 2 ヵ月連続で低下したほ
か、これに平仄を合わせる形で小売業が同▲1.5pt と低下した。これは、エネルギー価格の上
昇によってインフレ率のさらなる加速に対する懸念が高まっていることや、生活不安を理由 と
した各地での抗議活動の広がりが影響したとみられる。実際、10 月に入って大規模なデモや暴
動が発生したフランスの消費者信頼感指数は、同▲3.4pt と主要国の中でも目立って大きく前
月から悪化した。サービス業の景況感は依然として高水準ながら、同▲0.6pt と 6 ヵ月ぶりの
低下に転じたのも、消費者マインドの悪化や、それを背景とした需要の減少が下押し要因とな
った可能性が高い。

図表 3 ユーロ圏の景況感指数と実質 GDP 成長率（左） 、景況感指数の内訳（右）

（出所）Eurostat、欧州委員会より大和総研作成

一方、家計部門の弱含みとは対照的に、鉱工業の景況感はこのところ堅調さを増している。
鉱工業の景況感指数は、前月差＋0.5pt と 5 ヵ月連続で改善し、2023 年 4 月以来の高さとなっ
た。鉱工業の景況感の回復には、拡張財政による防衛支出・インフラ投資関連の需要の増加が

寄与しているとみられる。足元では長期金利が上昇ペースを速めており、これが設備投資や公
共投資の拡大を抑制する恐れはあるものの、積極的に財政を拡張 させるドイツを中心とした防
衛支出、インフラ投資がユーロ圏経済を下支えする構図は当面続くと見込まれる。 
先行きのリスク要因としては、引き続きエネルギー価格の動向、およびその影響の広がりが
焦点となる。今回の 7-9 月期の GDP 統計では、インフレ率が加速する中でも個人消費が底堅く
推移したことが確認された。だが、原油やガスなどのエネルギー価格は上昇が続いており、実
質所得や消費者マインドへの下押し圧力は、今後さらに強まる可能性がある。とりわけ冬季の
需要期にはエネルギー価格上昇による負担感が一層強まるとみられ、個人消費の下振れに警戒
する必要があるだろう。

3Q26 Review: 4 분기 실적 반등 기대감 Up  
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 600,000원(유지) 
 현재주가 292,500원(10/29)

 동사의 3분기 실적은 매출액 45.1조원(-3.4%yoy), 영업이익 2.68조원
(+5.5%yoy, OPM 5.9%), 지배이익 1.7조원(-24.6%yoy)을 기록하며 시장 
컨센서스를 상회. 도매 판매 감소, 원가 부담 증가 영향 있었으나 판매보증
충당금 축소와 원가 절감 활동을 통해 수익성 방어 
 실적 증감 요인: 영업이익 증감 요인은 물량 -1.11조원, 믹스/인센티브 -
0.57조원, 금융 -0.11조원, 환율 +1.28조원, 기타 +0.64조원 등. 원자재 
가격 상승으로 5천억원의 비용 증가 요인 있었으나 위기 대응 원가 절감 
활동으로 대부분 상쇄할 수 있었음. 전분기 대비 기말환율 급락함에 따라 
판매보증충당금 지난 해 1.26조원에서 340억원으로 약 1.23조원 감소. 판
보충 효과는 일회성에 그치겠지만 원가 절감 효과 컸던 점 긍정적  
 금융 부문: 금융부문 매출액은 전년비 2.8% 증가했지만 영업이익은 26% 
급감. 장기 금리 상승에 따른 이자 비용 증가와 기업금융 충당금 적립, 중고
차 관련 비용 확대가 수익성 악화의 주요 원인. 중고차 관련 비용은 EV 리
스 반납 차량의 매각 손실 확대에 기인한 것 
 4분기 전망: 동사는 국내 공장 정상화와 아반떼, 투싼, GV90, GV80 HEV 
등 주요 신차 효과를 통해 4분기 생산 확대와 주요 수출 시장 공급 회복이 
가능할 것으로 전망하였음. 원가 부담은 지속되나 물량 감소에 따른 영향에
서는 벗어날 수 있을 것. 원자재 가격 상승에 대응해 원가절감 프로그램 확
대와 신차 출시를 통한 인센티브 정상화를 통해 수익성 개선 지속할 계획 
 연간 가이던스 하향 조정: 글로벌 도매 판매 목표를 기존 416만대에서 
400만대로 하향 조정. 매출 성장률은 +1~2%, OPM 가이던스는 기존 
6.3~7.3%에서 5.5~6.5%로 하향 조정. 자동차 부문 투자 계획은 17.8조
원에서 16.0조원으로 축소. 핵심 R&D 투자는 유지하되 Capex 집행 시기 
및 우선 순위 조정할 계획 
 4분기 실적 반등 전망: 4분기 주요 신차(투싼, 아반떼, GV80 HEV, GV90) 
출시 본격화로 3분기까지의 부진을 만회할 수 있을 것으로 기대. 원가 부담 
은 비용 절감 활동을 통해 충분히 만회가 가능한 것으로 3분기 실적을 통
해 확인됨. 본격적인 주가 반등을 위해서는 판매 회복 이상의 주가 트리거 
필요할 것으로 보이나, 4분기 반전의 실마리가 나타나고 있다는 점에 주목. 
동사에 대한 투자의견 매수와 목표주가 60만원을 유지함

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

투자기간 및 투자등급/투자의견 비율 
종목추천 및 업종추천 투자기간: 12개월  (추천기준일 종가대비 추천종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함) 당사 투자의견 비율(%)

(26.10.29) 
292,500 
자동차업종

◼ 3Q26 비용절감/환율 효과로 우려 대비 양호한 실적 시현 
◼ 2027년 물량–환율 영향 상쇄되며 2H27 이익체력 개선 본격화 예상 
◼ 1) Physical AI(로봇/SDV) 경쟁력, 2) 주요국 점유율 반등에 주목

투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 50만원 하향(-6%) 
현대차 TP 50만원(시총 102조원)은 12MF EPS 41,978원에 Target PER 12배
적용하여 산출. 제한적인 실적 기대 반영해 타겟 밸류 하향함에 따라 TP 조정  
우려 대비 양호한 3Q26 실적으로 주가 하방 부담 완화. 주가의 추세적 상승 
위해서는 1) Physical AI(로봇/SDV) 경쟁력, 2) 주요국 점유율 반등 확인돼야 할 
것. 기저효과에 따른 물량 개선이 주가 저점 받쳐줄 것이나, 현재 밸류(2027년 
PER 9.1배vs 글로벌 피어 6.1배)에서 추세 상승 이끌긴 어려울 것이기 때문. 
이에, 자동차 본업 외 1) 한/미국 Physical AI 투자 계획 및 2) BD 공급망 구축 
/양산 가시화에 따른 주가 반등 기대

3Q26 매출 45조(YoY -3%, 이하 YoY), OP 2.7조(+6%), OPM 5.9% 시현 
3Q26 OP 추정(2.5조)/컨센(2.4조) 상회. 파업에 따른 생산/판매 차질 영향으로 
기대 제한적이었으나, 비용 절감/환율 영향으로 우려 대비 양호한 실적 시현. 
[자동차] 매출 33조(-10%), OP 2.1조(+25%), OPM 6.5%. 도매 판매 92.1만대
(-11%). 판매 부진과 원자재 부담에도 불구, 환효과(+1.3조)/비용절감(+0.5조)
로 실적 방어. 이외 YoY 변수별 영향은 물량(-1.1조)/믹스(-0.6조)/원자재(-
0.5조). [금융] 매출 9.2조(+28%), OP 4,710억(-18%), OPM 5.1%, [기타] 매출 
2.9조(+2%), OP 712억(-72%), OPM 2.5% 기록

2027년 매출 191조(YoY +2%, 이하 YoY), OP 12.7조(+15%), OPM 6.6% 전망 
기저효과 바탕으로 연결 도매 물량 YoY +5% 증가 예상하나, 원달러 환율 -
4% 하락(가정 환율 1,380원)하며 1H27 → 2H27로가며 이익체력 개선 예상 
(단위: 십억원, %)

### Page 5

DAISHIN SECURITIES

5  
  현대차 3Q26 실적 컨퍼런스콜 QnA 
Q. 금융부문 환율 변동에 따른 4Q26 매출 및 손익 영향 
- 금융부문 환율 영향은 현대차 실적에 미치는 환율 영향과 동일한 방향으로 발생

Q. 3Q26 금융부문 수익성 악화 요인 
- 장기금리 상승에 따른 조달 및 이자비용 증가가 주요인. 자산/부채 만기 매칭에 따른 
금리 상승 영향 반영 
- EV 중심 중고차 가격지수 소폭 하락 및 차량 반환에 따른 매각손실, 금리 상승에 따
른 연체율 및 신용비용 상승 
- 차량 판매가격 인하 영향은 없으며, 3Q26 도매 판매 감소에 따른 금융 영업수익 영향 
불가피했음

Q. 향후 주주환원 정책에서 배당 및 자사주 매입 우선순위 
- 기존 밸류업 정책: 최소 DPS 1만원/TPR 35% 제시. 연간 배당 약 2.6조원 → TPR 
35% 충족 위한 잔여분 자사주 매입/소각 활용 
- 경영환경 변화 고려하여 기존 밸류업 프로그램 전반의 주주가치 제고 방안 재검토중 
- 새로운 정책 확정 및 주주 소통 전까지 기존 약속인 최소 DPS 1만원 유지할 계획 
- 시장의 배당 및 자사주 매입 간 균형 요구를 인지하고 있으며, 주주 의견 수렴 후 주
주가치 제고 계획 공유 예정

Q. 3Q26 영업이익 증감 요인 중 기타 +6,400억 발생 원인 
- 월별 컨틴전시 활동을 통한 비용 최적화 및 재료비 절감 지속 추진 
- 4Q26 원가 절감 효과는 3Q26 대비 확대 예상

Q. 3Q26 영업외손익 감소 원인 
- 3Q26 환율 급락으로 외화 매출채권 등 외화자산 관련 외화환산손실 및 외환거래손실 
발생 
- 매출채권 인식 시점과 회수 시점 간 환율 차이에 따른 외환차손 발생 
- 지분법 대상 기업들의 3Q26 실적 부진에 따른 지분법손익 감소도 일부 영향. 대부분 
외환 관련 손실에서 발생

Q. 중국 OEM 국내 진출 확대에 따른 대응 및 신차/가격 전략 
- 중국 OEM 및 테슬라의 국내 M/S 확대 대응중. 다만, 잦은 가격 조정은 중고차 잔존
가치 및 고객가치 훼손할 수 있어 지양 
- 현대캐피탈의 캡티브 금융 경쟁력 활용. 잔존가치 유예할부 등을 통해 월 납입금을 낮
춰 중국 OEM 대비 경쟁력 확보 
- 서비스 네트워크 기반으로 경쟁사 대비 경쟁력 확보 노력 
- 국내 신차: 4Q26 아반떼 HEV, 투싼, GV90, G80 HEV, 싼타페 FL(2026.12월), G90 
FL 등 출시 예정 
- 북미: 4Q26 투싼, GV80 HEV 출시 및 27년 초 GV90 출시 예상 
- 유럽: 아이오닉3 판매 개시, 4Q26 투싼/베이온 등 주요 모델 출시 예정 
- 중국: 아이오닉 V / 인도: 베이온, 아이오닉 P 등 출시 예정 
- 신차 출시 계획 연도별로 다수 예정. 가격은 원가/경쟁사/소비자 가격 수용성 등을 고
려해 결정

Q. 3Q26 법인세비용 증가 원인 및 4Q26 전망 
- 일회성 요인보다는 법인별 손익 믹스 및 적용 세율 차이에 따른 영향 
- 4Q26 법인세 수준은 QoQ 큰 변동 없을 것으로 전망

Document 245: [207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 신규 공장이 채운 실적, 수주가 더할 모멘텀삼성바이오로직스(207940) 신규 공장이 채운 실적, 수주가 더_21961458

Source PDF: all report pdf/[207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 신규 공장이 채운 실적, 수주가 더할 모멘텀삼성바이오로직스(207940) 신규 공장이 채운 실적, 수주가 더_21961458.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-07
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
미국 록빌·5공장 가세로 3분기 기대치 부합 전망 
삼성바이오로직스의 2026년 3분기 연결 매출액은 1조 3,637억 원(+8.4% Y oY), 
영업이익은 6,092억 원(-3.8% YoY, 영업이익률 44.7%)으로 시장 기대치에 부합할 
전망이다. 5월 파업에 따른 생산 차질에도 1~4공장(총 60.4만 리터)의 풀가동과 
신규 공장의 매출 기여가 외형 성장을 뒷받침할 것으로 예상한다. 미국 록빌 
공장(6만 리터)은 3분기부터 매출 기여가 본격화되고, 5공장(18만 리터)도 
인증용(PPQ) 배치 생산을 통해 매출이 확대될 전망이다. 2분기에는 미국 공장·5공장 
관련 비용이 선반영됐으나, 3분기에는 매출 증가에 따른 고정비 부담 완화로 
영업이익률이 전분기 대비 0.3%p 상승할 것으로 예상한다. 
항체 중심 CDMO에서 펩타이드로 확장 
삼성바이오로직스의 2026년 신규 수주는 약 1.2조 원으로 전년 수주액의 약 20% 
수준에 그치고 있으나, 연말부터 내년까지 수주 모멘텀은 점차 강화될 것으로 
판단한다. 2025년 하반기 이후 미국의 의약품 관세 불확실성이 완화되며 글로벌 
제약사와의 논의가 늘어나고 있어, 신규 수주 계약으로 이어질 것으로 기대된다. 
11월 유상증자 납입이 마무리되면 6공장(18만 리터) 착공과 스위스 폴리펩타이드 
인수가 본격화되며 중장기 성장 기대감도 높아질 전망이다. 특히 폴리펩타이드 
인수가 연내 완료되면 2027년부터 연결 실적에 반영되는 동시에, 항체 의약품 
중심의 CDMO 사업을 GLP-1 등 펩타이드로 확장하는 계기가 될 전망이다. 
목표주가 200만 원으로 하향, 수주 회복이 관건 
삼성바이오로직스의 목표주가를 209만 원에서 200만 원으로 하향한다. 유상증자 
신주 발행에 따른 주당가치 4.7% 희석 효과를 반영했다. 폴리펩타이드 인수 효과는 
거래완료 이후 실적 추정치에 반영할 예정이다. 노조 파업 불확실성이 주가에 
부담으로 작용할 수 있어 신규 수주 모멘텀의 회복이 필요한 시점이다.

동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

Document 322: European resilience, American vigour (2026-10-30)

Source PDF: all report pdf/European resilience, American vigour (2026-10-30).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 2
Status: ok

### Page 1

Paris, 30 October 2026

EcoFlash

European resilience, American vigour

Eurozone growth continues to prove resilient, notwithstanding the volatility of the Irish figures. The Q3 data reinforce 
our forecast of 1.1% growth this year, which could even be slightly exceeded, as German and Spanish momentum shows 
no sign of fading. In France, the negative surprise (-0.1% q/q against a consensus of +0.1% q/q) stems from a new one-
off factor (the sharp fall in aerospace output caused by delays in input deliveries) and masks an encouraging rebound 
in household consumption. In the United  States, growth reached its highest level since Q2 2025 (0.8% q/q), driven by 
buoyant private domestic demand.

Eurozone: The resilience of growth is confirmed 
Eurozone GDP grew by 0.4% q/q in Q3  according to Eurostat's preliminary flash estimate, above both the consensus and our 
Nowcast (0.3%). The ever -volatile Irish figures contributed 0.2 pp to this outcome. Excluding Ireland, growth (0.2% q/q) 
nonetheless remains in line with the pace of recent quarters (0.2 -0.3% q/q on average). This illustrates the Eurozone's capacity 
to withstand the current shocks, thanks in particular to the strength of Spain (+0.07 pp contribution to the q/q change in Eurozone’s 
GDP) and the Netherlands (+0.04 pp), and to Germany's gradual recovery (+0.06 pp). France (-0.03 pp) and Italy (zero growth), 
by contrast, posted disappointing results, although in France's case these partly reflect temporary factors (aerospace output). The 
growth carry-over for 2026 now stands at 1.0%, which supports our forecast of 1.1% in annual average terms and even points to 
upside risk. The European Commission's October surveys remain upbeat, with a further improvement in industrial activity 
expectations. 
The German recovery continues . As anticipated by our  Nowcast, real GDP grew by 0.2% q/q in Q3 (after 0.3% in Q2).  
According to Destatis, consumption (private and public) increased slightly, while investment edged down. Export momentum 
slowed markedly compared with Q2. This is Germany's fourth consecutive quarter of growth. 
Italian GDP surprised on the downside, stagnating in Q3 (against a consensus of 0.1%).  According to Istat, this reflects a 
decline in value added in industry, offset by increases in services and agriculture. The positive contribution from domestic demand 
was reportedly cancelled out by a negative contribution from net trade. 
Spanish growth remained robust in Q3, in line with expectations (0.6% q/q, after 0.7% in Q2),  driven mainly by domestic 
demand (0.5 pp contribution) and, to a lesser extent, by net exports (0.1 pp). Consumption remains the main engine of growth 
(+0.6% q/q for private consumption and +0.9% for public consumption). Investment rose by 0.7%. Export gr owth (1.3%) once 
again outpaced import growth (1.1%) this quarter. All sectors except construction recorded positive changes in value added, with 
industry and services both up 0.7%. 
France: A sharp drop in aerospace output prevents a return to growth in Q3 
GDP contracted by 0.1% q/q in Q3 (after stagnating in Q2), weighed down by a sharp fall in aerospace output (-4.5% q/q 
in the production of other transport equipment, which includes aerospace). This steep decline dragged down goods production 
(down 1.2% q/q) and explains the sharp contraction in exports ( -1.7% q/q) and business investment (-0.4% q/q). Net trade thus 
subtracted 0.6 pp from GDP (imports were flat over the same period). Conversely, household consumption surprised on the 
upside (+0.4% q/q), boosted by purchases of electric vehicles (registrations up 112% y/y in Q3). Public consumption 
(+0.3% q/q) also supported growth, as did activity in market services (+0.3% q/q).  Despite the sharp drop in aerospace 
output in Q3, Airbus has confirmed its delivery targets for the year (with engine supply problems expected to be resolved), pointing 
to a strong rebound in growth in Q4 (0.3% q/q according to our forecasts). Overall, gr owth is expected to reach 0.3% in 2026 , 
before picking up to 0.8% in 2027. 
United States: Growth at a one-year high  
GDP growth picked up in Q3 to 3.4% at a seasonally adjusted annualised rate ( AR; i.e. 0.8% q/q), from 2.2% in Q2 
(0.6% q/q). This is the fastest pace since Q3 2025, confirming that activity is almost immune to the energy shock. Private domestic 
demand remains particularly strong. Private non-residential investment accelerated to 12.7% AR, a performance unmatched since 
the post-Covid reopening. AI-related components are driving this figure (18.5% AR), but other types of investment are also rising 
for a second consecutive quarter (3.8% AR) after contracting uninterruptedly throughout 2025. Household consumption, steady 
at 2.5% AR in real terms, is growing faster than it did before inflation picked up again, but at the cost of a significant drop in the 
saving rate (3.8% at the end of the quarter, down from 4.4% at the end of the previous quarter). The United States nonetheles s 
continues to "import its growth": net trade is weighing on GDP owing to a surge in imports (19.7% AR), driven by AI -related 
components – IT, semiconductors and telecommunications (103.8% AR).

EUROZONE: IMPROVING BUSINESS SENTIMENT, DRIVEN BY TECH, IS SPREADING TO OTHER SECTORS
Stéphane Colliac & Tarik Rharrab
*LATEST OBSERVATION: 5 OCTOBER 2026  **ESTIMATE
The September PMI surveys confirm that growth in the Eurozone is getting stronger, boosted in particular by new export orders, which are
still rising. Sectors that were already performing well – technology and capital goods (including for defence) – are continuing to grow, while

those that had been struggling until now – aeronautics, consumer goods and financial services – are seeing their production indices recover.
This strong performance is particularly noteworthy, given that it has not yet been undermined by higher inflation.
The heatmap shows the PMI index readings: a reading above 50 indicates expansion, while a reading below 50 signals contraction compared to the previous
month. For each indicator, sectors are ranked in descending order based on their September 2026 reading.

Shades of red represent values above 50, while shades of blue represent values below 50: the intensity of the colour increases the further away they are
from this threshold.
Production growth spreads to more sectors
The PMI surveys on business sentiment confirm that business activity in the Eurozone is expanding at a faster pace and spreading to more sectors.
Household-consumption-related sectors (goods and services), which were adversely affected in the spring by the outbreak of the conflict in the Middle

East, do not appear to be negatively impacted by the rising inflation observed since August. The aeronautics sector, where production has mainly been
limited by supply constraints, is also showing signs of improvement.

These trends, although still moderate, come alongside a drive to upgrade equipment, fuelled by investment in digital technology, electrification
(which is supporting the automotive sector) and defence. The momentum in technology goods and services further strengthened in September.
The production index for computer and electronics hit a five-year high, while the software services production index stayed close to its July peak.

Financial services are also enjoying a significant improvement, reflecting the healthy lending momentum despite interest rates starting to rise
(see our analysis).
Growth driven by exports
Exports drove growth in the first half of 2026, both across the Eurozone as a whole (see our analysis) and among its key members (notably
Germany). This momentum is also increasing, as indicated by the ‘new export orders’ component of the various PMI indices. As was the case with

production, there has been a particularly noteworthy increase for capital goods (technology and machinery) and related services, particularly digital
services. This trend is playing out more broadly, as, in September, new export orders rose across all sub-sectors, with the exception of intermediate
goods and consumer goods, where contraction recently eased. There has been a significant improvement in the transport sector, particularly in
the aeronautics industry, which has major markets in Asia. Recent data on French exports show that aeronautics exports to this region are rising

once again.
The convergence between rising production and increasing export-order backlogs shows that Eurozone growth is based on an increasingly solid
foundation. Inflationary pressures are not holding it back for the time being.

Germany: Politics vs economy – round two
2026 was the year of unexpected resilience, despite a continuing
struggle to decide and actually implement structural reforms. Still,
the economy delivered its strongest growth outcome since 2022,
driven chieﬂy by longer-term ﬁscal support for defence and

infrastructure. Some of the industrial momentum should carry over
into 2027, as will ﬁscal stimulus.
The main question, however, is whether ﬁscal stimulus will once
again be enough to oﬀset the lack of competitiveness-enhancing
reforms. Whether the government can implement already
announced reforms, enact further-reaching reforms to reduce

actually turn into a recovery. For now, we expect 2027 growth to

France: Weak growth keeps the ﬁscal
problem centre stage
French GDP growth is expected to reach only 0.5% in 2027, after
0.3% in 2026. Weak conﬁdence will limit household spending, while
higher ﬁnancing costs and uncertainty about future taxes and
public expenditure will discourage investment. The labour market is
likely to soften further, providing little support to domestic demand.

This anaemic growth will continue to make the ﬁscal arithmetic
much harder.
Even if a 2027 budget is adopted, weaker tax revenues and rising
interest costs are likely to leave the deﬁcit at around 5.5% of GDP,
above the government’s 5% target and far too high to stabilise
public debt. Political uncertainty will also remain high ahead of the
presidential election and possibly beyond as parliamentary
elections are likely to follow. Given the high likelihood that no stable

majority will emerge, the risk premium on French bonds will likely
remain elevated in the ﬁrst half of 2027.
Italy: Growth set to decelerate,
notwithstanding a mildly expansionary
budget
Italy’s economy should continue to grow in 2027, but at a slower
pace. Domestic demand will remain the key driver, despite higher
policy rates, elevated inﬂation and the recovery plan’s expiry.
Unless energy prices turn lower soon, inﬂation is likely to stay

Document 364: New Zealand 2026 General Election_ Preliminary results and next steps (2026-11-10)

Source PDF: all report pdf/New Zealand 2026 General Election_ Preliminary results and next steps (2026-11-10).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-10
Pages: 9
Status: ok

### Page 1

1
Westpac New Zealand Economics
• As polls suggested, the election was a nailbiter
that has not yet been resolved. Election night
results show a lead to the centre-right on
paper in a significantly enlarged parliament.
• The centre-right bloc of National, NZ First
and ACT won 67 seats. The centre-left bloc
of Labour, Greens and Te Pāti Māori won 57
seats. There was a swing away from National
to its coalition partners while the centre left
grouping picked up a few seats.

• The Opportunity Party delivered on its polling
and will enter parliament with at least 7 seats,
reducing what could have been a larger swing
to the centre-left grouping.
• The size of parliament will expand by around 9
seats as National held onto a large share of its
electorate votes, entitling it to more seats than
implied by its party vote share. As a result,
a bloc would require 66 seats to command a
majority in parliament.
• Several senior National ministers have not

been re-elected, including Finance Minister
Nicola Willis. She will continue in a caretaker
role but will need to be replaced in any new
centre-right government.
• Significant uncertainty about the result exists.
National might lose perhaps two marginal
electorates in the final analysis. There are
400,000 special votes to count which may
shift the list seat allocations as these tend
to reduce the share of the centre-right bloc
by 1.6-1.7ppts. Hence, when all is said and

done, the centre-right’s 10 seat advantage on
election night could reduce by 1-4 seats.
• Should this play out the centre-right would not
have sufficient seats to reach a majority in an
enlarged house and would require the support
of the Opportunity Party to govern.
• The final election outcome will not be
known until 27 November. Serious coalition
negotiations likely won’t begin until then
and delays could occur. Should recounts be
required, some further delay is possible.

• Based on these results, we see a higher
probability of a centre-right government
ultimately emerging that likely will require
the support of the Opportunity Party in some
form. The Opportunity Party likely won’t enter
Cabinet but may offer support on confidence
and supply in exchange for policy concessions.
• But a centre-left government can’t be ruled
out if special votes heavily favour the centre
left bloc, and especially if an agreement
between the Opportunity Party and the centre-

right parties can’t be reached.
• Regardless of the final count, the results
suggest a government with weak support and
ongoing vulnerabilities. Hence the prospect of
controversial large changes may be modest.
For a centre-right government that might
limit the extent of fiscal consolidation, while
the centre left’s plans for larger scale tax and
spending initiatives could be trimmed back.
• A centre-right government won’t change the
RBNZ mandate while a centre-left government

likely still will. A new finance minister will be
required with the possibility of ACT leader
David Seymour taking on that role.

New Zealand 2026 General Election:
Preliminary results and next steps.
9 Nov 2026 |  Westpac Economics Team | westpac.co.nz/economics | economics@westpac.co.nz
Kelly Eckhold, Chief Economist | +64 9 348 9382 | +64 21 786 758 | kelly.eckhold@westpac.co.nz

### Page 2

2
Westpac New Zealand Economics
The result.
The preliminary results of the 2026 General Election,
based on the election night count, are set out in the table
above. In summary:
• The centre-right bloc of National, NZ First and ACT
won 67 seats.
• The centre-left bloc of Labour, Greens and Te Pāti
Māori won 57 seats.
• The Opportunity Party delivered on its polling and will
enter parliament with 7 seats.
These results indicate a swing away from National to its

coalition partners while the centre-left grouping picked
up a few seats. The big gainer was the Opportunity Party
who perhaps took a big share of what otherwise could
have been a larger swing to the centre-left grouping.
The size of parliament will expand by around nine seats,
as National held onto a large share of its electorate
votes and hence has more seats than it would have been
entitled to based on its party vote share. As a result, a
governing bloc would require 66 seats to command a

majority in parliament.
Several of the electorate seats are very tight (see Table
2), so election night majorities could easily be overturned
once special votes are counted. Of particular relevance
is the seat of Wairarapa, where the National candidate is
leading the Labour candidate by just 139 votes. If Labour
were to win that seat (specials favoured Labour in 2020
but National in 2023), all else equal the centre-right seat
count would decline to 66 seats in what would become

a 130-seat Parliament – just enough to command a
majority. But if either NZ First or ACT were to lose a single
party vote seat to the left bloc once specials are counted
then that majority would disappear.
On that score, historically the election night results have
tended to overstate the support of the centre-right bloc
and understate the support for parties on the left of
the political spectrum. For example, over the previous
three elections (2017, 2020, 2023), the current centre-

right bloc’s vote share has declined 1.9%, 1.7% and 1.6%
respectively following the counting of special votes, with
the National Party losing 2 seats on each occasion once
the official results were declared (Labour, the Greens and
Te Pāti Māori have benefited). On this occasion, given its
overhang, a lower party vote share would not lower the
National Party’s final vote count, although it could alter
the size of its overhang and thus the number of seats

required to command a majority. In recent elections both
NZ First and ACT have seen their vote shares decline
following the counting of special votes.
The process from here.
Official results.
The official results will be declared on 27 November,
adding the results of an estimated 400,000 special votes
(with around a quarter of these cast overseas) to the
2.41m votes counted on election night.
We expect discussions between potential coalition
partners will begin immediately to start sketching out the

parameters of a coalition agreement. But given the tight
margins of the current vote count, no firm positions will
be agreed until the results are declared.
The writ and recounts.
After 27 November, the writ is scheduled to be returned
on 3 December – formally certifying who has been
elected. However, that date is subject to any recounts

### Page 3

3
Westpac New Zealand Economics
that might be requested. Parliament is required to meet
no later than 14 January (six weeks after the return of the
writ), providing an opportunity for a new government
to test that it has the confidence of the House. It is
worth noting that Parliament can be summoned and
begin functioning even if coalition negotiations are still
occurring. If no coalition or confidence-and-supply
arrangement has been reached by then, negotiations can

simply continue and the incumbent Government remains
in office under the caretaker convention (Ministers in
the outgoing government retain executive authority
temporarily, even if they are no longer MPs).
Given that the Pre-Election Economic and Fiscal
Update was released in late September there is no legal
requirement for the Treasury to publish a Half Year
Economic and Fiscal Update (HYEFU) this year. And given
that it will be some weeks before a government is formed,

it seems unlikely that there will be a government in place
that would request that a HYEFU be produced despite
there being no statutory requirement (certainly there
would be little time to incorporate policy changes that
fall out of new coalition agreements, thus diminishing
its value). Once a government is formed, it will have an
opportunity to lay out its immediate priorities in the
Budget Policy Statement (BPS), which by law needs to
be released before the end of March next year. The new

government could decide to release an interim fiscal
update with the BPS if that was thought necessary.
Potential coalition outcomes.
We see three main likely outcomes:
• A three-party majority coalition between National, ACT
and NZ First.
• A three-party majority coalition between National, ACT
and NZ First with the support of the Opportunity Party
on confidence and supply.
• Some coalition of Labour, the Greens, Opportunity and
Te Pāti Māori, potentially with Opportunity and Te Pāti

Māori sitting outside the cabinet.
Our preview of the election discussed the policies of
the main political parties, while the figure on page 5
highlights some points of commonality with the centre-
right and centre-left blocs.
National, ACT and NZ First share a broadly pro-
development and deregulatory agenda, including support
for greater resource development, fewer regulatory
barriers and compulsory or expanded KiwiSaver. Beyond
that common core, National and ACT align particularly

closely on lower taxes and deregulation, while National
and NZ First share an emphasis on infrastructure, regional
development and law and order. ACT and NZ First favour
tighter immigration settings and tighter welfare policies.
The differences are clearest at the edges: ACT advocates
deeper tax cuts, smaller government and more market-
led provision, while NZ First places greater emphasis
on economic sovereignty, regional development and
targeted support for working New Zealanders. National

occupies more of the middle ground, combining fiscal
discipline and a pro-business agenda with increased
infrastructure and housing supply.
The Opportunity Party presents itself as a middle
alternative that can work with the left or the right
groupings. They have five bottom line policies they say
they need to support any new government. These include
competition reform through enhanced Commerce
Commission powers, more restrictions on political
lobbyists and donors, a ten-year plan of investment in the

healthcare sector, an end to bottom trawling in inshore
areas and a phase out elsewhere and for the Government
to recommit to New Zealand’s Paris Agreement
obligations. Their success in meeting these bottom
lines with a new government and their flexibility around
these requirements will be key to their ability to form an
agreement with either side. There is better congruence
of the Opportunity Party’s policies with the left than the
right – so progress here will be critical to watch should

their support be required to form a new government. We
suspect they will be very motivated to avoid the need for
a new election via a hung parliament should push come
to shove.

Labour, the Greens and Opportunity share support
for increased public and social investment, a broader
tax base, greater infrastructure investment, stronger
competition policy, cheaper public transport and a
more active role for government. Labour and the Greens
overlap particularly on higher taxation, public investment
and stronger climate and environmental policy, while
Labour and Opportunity share an emphasis on social

investment and higher public-service wages. The Greens
and Opportunity both favour broader taxation and
stronger climate action, although their preferred tax
instruments differ. Their distinctive priorities are also
clear: Labour places more weight on worker protections
and restoring the RBNZ’s dual mandate; the Greens on
environmental protection, progressive taxation and Te
Tiriti; and Opportunity on land-value taxation, housing
infrastructure, productivity, innovation and evidence-

based policy.

熱門議題 / 投資關鍵
《AI晶片與相關硬體獲利持續創新高》
根據機構LSEG統計，受到AI晶片、記憶體、硬碟等
科技公司獲利優於預期，機構上調科技產業4Q26、
1Q27獲利年增率到56.2%、54.6%，本行樂觀看待
科技產業發展後勢 。10月份重要科技硬體晶圓代工
龍頭台積電3Q26法說表示，受益於強勁AI需求與2
奈米開始貢獻營收，公司3Q26營收與獲利均優於市
場預期。3Q26高效運算與智慧手機營收比重分別達
68%、21%。高效運算營收年增高達 53%、智慧手
機年減10%。AI代理與開源模型普及帶動先進製程

用量 ， 台 積 電 預 估4Q26 美 元 營 收 將 年 增62% ，
2026年美元營收預估達44%。由於AI晶片成長高於
60%，預估台積電2027年營收與獲利仍將續創新高。

重點摘要
• 雲端巨頭資本支出持平 ，伺
服器仍優於消費終端
《雲端巨頭資本支出持平 ，伺服器仍優於消費終端 》
3Q26財報顯示 ，谷哥 、亞馬遜 、微軟與 Meta在
2026年資本支出目標合計達 7,300億美元 ，較上季
略減25億美元(-0.3%)，減少主要是微軟外部第三方
算力產能延遲導致資本支出目標下修 50億美元，與
Meta上調25億美元相互抵消 。雖然雲端巨頭支出
持平但資料中心晶片需求仍強勁。英特爾3Q26財報
顯示，受益於資料中心與 AI事業帶動營收達 165億
美元、年增21%。其中資料中心與人工智慧相關營
收73億美元續創歷史新高、年增78%，2027年相關
晶片仍將供不應求 。反觀個人電腦營收 81億美元、

年減5%。英特爾預估 2026年個人電腦出貨量將年
減12%、2027年受到記憶體與儲存供給影響預估將
續減10%。
受到上半年消費者預期終端售價上漲提前換機，
3Q26個人電腦與智慧手機出貨均呈現大幅下滑。根
據IDC統計，3Q26全球個人電腦出貨量達6,270萬
台，較2025年同期衰退20%。同樣受到記憶體漲價
與供應短缺衝擊的智慧手機3Q26出貨量2.6億台，
較2025年同期衰退22%。由於未來記憶體與儲存價
格小幅調漲以及供給受限下，消費終端價格仍將維
持高價位。因此，IDC預估個人電腦與智慧手機
2027年將高機率維持衰退，預估要2028年出貨量
才有望復甦。
資料來源：公司，2026/10/30，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10/30

吸納量
重點摘要
3
《類比晶片終端需求上升 ，工業、資料中心占比較
高供應商營收更具成長性》
德州儀器(TXN)3Q26營收60.6億美元、EPS 2.62美
元雙雙優於預期，4Q26財測營收58-63億美元，中
位數較市場普遍預期高出 6%，反映資料中心市場
需求持續成長 ，使營收預測優於往年季節性回落 。
以3Q26終端市場來看 ，工業營收年增超過 40%、
季增約10%，成長來自航太國防 、能源基礎設施等
領域；車用營收年增約 15%、季增約10%，受益於
客戶庫存水準降低及電動車占比提高；資料中心營
收則年增約95%、季增20%，為成長最快的終端市
場。在營運方面，產能利用率提高以及2027年潛在
的價格調漲可能進一步擴大獲利成長空間 ，儘管資

本支出可能高於2026年的20~30億美元，但顯示終
端需求正擴大成長。
恩智浦(NXPI)3Q26營收37.9億美元、EPS 4.20美元
優於預期 ，但4Q26財測營收中位數 38.5億美元略
低於市場預期 。以3Q26終端市場來看 ，車用營收
20.7億美元、年增13%，成長主要來自軟體定義汽
車(SDV)產品貢獻；工業與物聯網營收 8.02億美元 、
年增39%，主要由資料中心 、工廠自動化和醫療保
健等產業帶動；通訊基礎設施營收 5.03億美元、年
增54%，得益於資料中心業務成長 。恩智浦財報結
果顯示各終端市場需求持續上升 ，惟成長速度較慢
的車用營收占比超過五成 ，未來成長速度可能不及
工業和資料中心營收占比更高的同業。
• 德州儀器 4Q26 財 測 營 收 較
市場預期高出6%，反映資料
中心市場需求大幅成長。
• 德儀產能利用率提高及 2027
年潛在的價格調漲可能進一
步擴大獲利成長空間。
• 恩智浦3Q26營收、EPS優於
市場預期 ，各終端市場營收
皆呈年增。
